We ask what such a probe measures, in a construction chosen to make the question sharp: a ring of token cells resampled in place by the model's own windowed conditional p_r(x_i | x_{i+-r}). The substrate is Glauber dynamics on token sequences and is not new; what we change is the coupling. Advancing two rings that differ in one token under common random numbers makes undamaged copies diverge by exactly zero, so damage spreading becomes measurable where a maximal coupling gives mixing times instead. The answer is that it measures two different things at once, in readings that look alike. Some quantities are fixed by the construction: the damage light cone is kinematic, and the radius scaling of the token-space Lyapunov exponent lambda_ca(r) is model-invariant across 19 models and two scale ladders spanning 70x. Others genuinely track the model: lambda_ca crosses zero at a reproducible point in training, and the attractor share ranks models consistently however the lattice is built. Left undistinguished, the first kind is readily mistaken for the second -- we did so ourselves for four months, and report a phase transition we measured to three decimal places that belongs to the probe rather than to any language model. We give the test that separates them: hold the construction fixed and vary the model, or hold the model fixed and vary the construction, and see which readings move. We validate the instrument by reproduction first, recovering a Domany-Kinzel damage field bit-exactly against an independent prediction, and we report the estimator failures that this discipline caught -- four retracted verdicts, each on a quantity that looked like a measurement. The methodology ships as a package.

</details>


### [164] [ConRub-Med: Reinforcement Learning with Consensus Rubrics for Open-Ended Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10996)
*Taojie Zhu,Yuan Xia,Tao Sun,Yizhi Wang,Yan Chen,Qunshan He,Tian Guan,Jian Wang,Jinjie Gu,Junwei Liu,Yonghong He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ConRub-Med方法，通过多模型独立生成、单独评审和保留三方共识的原子准则来构建医学评分量规，并采用三态评分（正确覆盖、缺失、错误）与GRPO策略优化，在九个医学基准上排名第一，超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在数学和编码领域因可自动验证答案而有效，但开放式医学问题缺乏廉价的自动验证器。医生撰写的评分量规临床基础强但成本高，模型生成的量规虽然可扩展但需保证质量，因此需要在从量规构建到策略优化的过程中保留有用的判别信息。

Method: ConRub-Med包含三个组件：1) 三个异构语言模型独立为每个提示提出原子准则，由独立模型评审并仅保留三方语义支持的准则；2) 采用三态评分区分正确覆盖、缺失信息和错误陈述，错误给予负分而非零分；3) 在GRPO组中若所有响应获得相同最终奖励，使用成对评判器提供序列优势（仅当两个候选顺序一致时），无平局组使用标准GRPO。

Result: 盲法研究中医务专家评价ConRub-Med完整流水线生成的评估面板临床相关性高于单个生成器。在九个基准中六个排名第一，取得最高医学和泛化平均值。在HealthBench-Hard上达到38.98±1.04分（比InfiMed-ORBIT的33.60有显著提升），使用了5,166个提示的评分量规数据集。

Conclusion: ConRub-Med通过多模型共识机制和三态评分策略有效解决了医学开放性问题中奖励验证的挑战，实现了低成本、高质量的医学评分量规构建与策略优化，显著提升了开放医学模型的性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has been especially effective in mathematics and coding, where answers can be checked automatically. Many open-ended medical questions lack comparably cheap outcome verifiers: responses may be partly correct, incomplete, or contain clinically consequential errors. Rubrics written or validated by physicians offer strong clinical grounding, but involving experts in every instance is costly. Model-generated rubrics make this supervision scalable. We introduce ConRub-Med to preserve useful distinctions as rubric feedback moves from construction to policy optimization. For each prompt, three heterogeneous language models propose atomic criteria independently; a separate model reviews them, retaining only criteria with semantic support from all three generators. Three-State scoring distinguishes correct coverage, missing information, and incorrect claims. Errors receive negative rather than zero credit. When every response in a complete Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) group receives the same final reward, a pairwise judge provides sequence advantages only if both candidate orders agree, without changing the scalar rewards. Groups without ties use vanilla GRPO. In a blinded study matched by question, two medical experts rate panels from the full pipeline as more clinically relevant than panels produced by one generator. Across the evaluated open models, ConRub-Med ranks first on six of nine benchmarks and achieves the highest medical and generalization averages. Using the resulting rubric dataset of 5,166 prompts, it scores $38.98 \pm 1.04$ (mean $\pm$ SD) on HealthBench-Hard, compared with InfiMed-ORBIT's 33.60 with 8,000 samples and 37.30 with 28,000.

</details>


### [165] [On the Limitations of Cross-Lingual Consistency in Multilingual Text-to-image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11002)
*Sicheng Zhang,Zhonghao Yan,Binzhu Xie,Shi Qiu,Muzammal Naseer,Naveed Akhtar,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了LingT2I基准，覆盖10种广泛使用语言、包含33K提示词，用于评估文本到图像生成中的跨语言效果（包括内容生成和文本渲染），并揭示了语言不平等和与语言相关的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到图像生成研究主要集中在英语环境，跨语言性能差距和语言特定效应尚未得到充分探索，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了覆盖10种语言、33K提示词的LingT2I基准，从内容生成和文本渲染两个维度进行跨语言评估，并分析语言因素及其文化背景对模型输出的系统影响。

Result: 发现了跨语言评估维度上的语言不平等现象和语言依赖的权衡，揭示了多种语言依赖的生成模式，展示语言因素和文化背景如何系统性影响模型输出。

Conclusion: 该基准和分析为研究T2I生成中的跨语言行为奠定了基础，有助于开发更稳健和包容的模型。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) generation has achieved remarkable progress in recent years. However, existing research has largely focused on English-only settings, leaving cross-lingual performance gaps and language-specific effects insufficiently explored. To fill this gap, we introduce LingT2I, a benchmark covering 10 widely used languages with 33K prompts, designed to evaluate cross-lingual effects in both content generation and text rendering. Building on this benchmark, we conduct a comprehensive cross-lingual analysis, uncovering linguistic inequality and language-dependent trade-offs across evaluation dimensions. Beyond quantitative evaluation, we further reveal a range of language-dependent generation patterns, highlighting how linguistic factors and their corresponding cultural contexts systematically impact model outputs. Our benchmark and analysis provide a foundation for studying cross-lingual behavior in T2I generation and facilitate the development of more robust and inclusive models. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/RISys-Lab/LingT2I.

</details>


### [166] [Templated or fully Synthetic? Prompt construction as a confound in measuring LLM political stance beyond writing assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11008)
*Ilias Chalkidis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出用完全合成（LLM生成的）提示词替代模板化提示词来检测LLM政治立场，以提高生态效度。研究发现LLM生成的提示词与真实提示词同样真实，且比模板化提示词更清晰传达意图和立场，并指出模板化提示词会高估模型在特定方向上的立场倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM政治立场检测依赖于为人类设计的封闭式多项选择调查问题，缺乏真实人类-AI交互的多样性和细微差别，且易受sandbagging影响。虽然IssueBench用模板化提示词缓解了这些问题，但模板化提示词仍缺乏真实提示词的细微差别，容易被识别为评估产物。因此需要更真实、更自然的提示词来评估LLM的政治立场。

Method: 在IssueBench基础上扩展了两个新任务（信息寻求和观点分享），并提出使用完全合成（LLM生成的）提示词：以真实提示词为种子，在详细指令下由LLM生成。通过人类和LLM标注者评估真实、模板化和LLM生成的提示词的生态效度，覆盖3个高度争议的政策议题和3个近期地缘政治冲突。

Result: 人类和LLM标注者均认为LLM生成的提示词与真实提示词同等真实且明显比模板化提示词更真实；LLM生成的提示词能更清晰地传达预期意图和立场。此外，LLM区分模板化提示词与另外两类提示词的能力远强于人类。案例研究显示，模板化和LLM生成的提示词对同一模型产生系统不同的立场估计，在中立框架下差异最明显，模板化提示词会高估模型在填充文本所编码方向上的倾向。

Conclusion: LLM生成的合成提示词是比模板化提示词更优越的LLM政治立场检测工具，具有更高的生态效度，能更准确地反映LLM在真实交互中的政治倾向。

Abstract: Political stance detection in LLMs has long been dominated by closed-ended, multiple-choice political survey questions---originally designed for humans, and thus lacks the realism and nuance of human-AI interactions in the wild, while also being susceptible to sandbagging. The recent IssueBench framework substantially mitigates these limitations with templated prompts anchored in real-world chat logs. Given the rise in non-work-related use of GenAI assistants, we extend IssueBench beyond writing assistance to include two additional tasks, information seeking and opinion sharing. We argue that templated prompts still lack the nuance of real ones, especially for open-ended tasks, and remain recognisable as evaluation artefacts. We propose the use of fully synthetic (LLM-generated) prompts, produced under detailed instructions with real prompts as seeds. We assess the ecological validity of real, templated, and LLM-generated prompts in a small-scale study covering 3 highly contested policy issues and 3 recent geopolitical conflicts. Human and LLM annotators rank LLM-generated prompts as no less realistic than real ones and clearly more realistic than templated ones, and find that they carry their intended intent and stance more clearly; the LLMs separate templated prompts from the other two far more sharply than the humans do. In a case study, templated and LLM-generated prompts yield systematically different stance estimates for the same model, most visibly under neutral framings, where templated prompts overstate the model's leaning in the direction encoded by the topic-and-stance text (filler) slotted into their templates.

</details>


### [167] [Data Attribution of Emergent Misalignment with Persona Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11025)
*Clemens Vetter,David Kaczér,Lucie Flek,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）模型差异分析，发现微调语言模型引发的"突发性错位"（EM）与特定的persona特征（如越狱人格、讽刺、欺骗、操纵）相关，这些特征可通过干预单独控制错位行为。尽管预训练文档中蕴含语义相关的叙事内容，但仅靠人类文本无法可靠诱发EM，而由相同内容生成的合成指令-响应对则有效且跨模型族迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将"突发性错位"归因于预训练期间获取的persona特征，但对此类特征的来源缺乏理解：哪些预训练文档激活这些特征，自然产生的人类文本是否足以诱发EM。

Method: 使用基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的模型差异方法，对四个开源权重模型进行分析；通过干预（steering）单个特征双向控制EM；将因果特征归因到一百万预训练网页文档语料库；对比人类文本与合成指令-响应对在微调中诱发EM的效果。

Result: 与越狱人格、讽刺、欺骗和操纵相关的特征被错位微调放大，安全相关和助手身份特征被抑制。干预单个特征可在对齐模型上诱导高达62%的错位率（超过错位微调本身的35%），并使错位模型恢复到接近基线水平。语义相关的人类文档微调不可靠诱发EM，而合成指令-响应对则能可靠诱发且跨模型族迁移。

Conclusion: 语义相关性本身不足以诱发EM，响应结构或模型生成的措辞在诱导突发性错位中起关键作用。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM) is the phenomenon where fine-tuning a language model on a narrow task leads to harmful behavior in unrelated domains. A leading mechanistic account attributes EM to persona features: latent directions acquired during pre-training that misaligned fine-tuning amplifies. We ask where these features come from: which pre-training documents activate them, and whether naturally occurring human-written text suffices to induce EM. Using Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) based model diffing across four open-weight models, we find that features related to jailbreak personas, sarcasm, deception, and manipulation are amplified by misalignment fine-tuning, while safety-relevant and assistant-identity features are suppressed. Steering individual features controls EM in both directions: it induces misalignment rates of up to 62% in aligned models -- exceeding the 35% reached by misalignment fine-tuning itself -- and re-aligns misaligned models to near-baseline misalignment rates. Attributing the causal features to a corpus of one million pre-training web documents retrieves semantically relevant narratives about villainous characters, domination, and harmful agency. However, fine-tuning on these human-written documents does not reliably induce EM, even after reformatting into assistant-style responses, whereas synthetic instruction-response pairs derived from the same content do -- and transfer across model families. Semantic relevance alone is therefore not sufficient: response structure or model-generated phrasing plays an important role in inducing EM.

</details>


### [168] [myMediWhisper: Construction of Burmese Medical Speech Corpus and Whisper Fine-Tuning for Clinical Dialogue ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11036)
*Ye Kyaw Thu,Ye Bhone Lin,Thura Aung,Htet Arkar,Myat Oo Swe,Thet Htet San,Min Thiha Tun,Thazin Myint Oo,Thepchai Supnithi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个缅甸语医疗语音识别框架，基于28小时由母语者录制和验证的高质量语料库，对Whisper模型进行全量微调和LoRA参数高效微调，并通过数据增强提升噪声环境下的鲁棒性。最佳系统myMediWhisper-Medium在无增强条件下达到23.44%的SOTA词错误率（WER），超越了更大的通用领域模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Whisper模型受益于大规模多语言预训练，但其在缅甸语医疗语音上的性能仍然有限，需要专门构建面向该场景的语音识别系统。

Method: 构建28小时由母语者录制并验证的缅甸语医疗语音语料库；使用全量微调（FFT）和基于LoRA的参数高效微调（PEFT）对Whisper模型进行微调；在受控噪声和模拟室内声学条件下应用波形级和频谱级数据增强来评估鲁棒性。

Result: 数据增强虽然降低了干净语音的性能，但在FFT和PEFT设置下显著提升了噪声和混响环境中的鲁棒性。最佳系统为无增强的全量微调myMediWhisper-Medium，WER达到23.44%，超越更大的通用领域微调模型。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，针对缅甸语医疗场景构建专用微调数据集和模型可以有效提升识别性能，且较小的领域专用模型能够超越更大的通用模型，数据集已公开于Huggingface仓库。

Abstract: Although Whisper models benefit from large-scale multilingual pre-training, their performance on Burmese medical speech remains limited. This work presents a Burmese medical speech recognition framework built on a high-quality 28-hour corpus recorded and validated by native speakers. We fine-tune Whisper models using full fine-tuning (FFT) and parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) with LoRA. To evaluate robustness, we apply waveform- and spectrogram-level data augmentation under controlled noise and simulated room acoustics. While augmentation reduces performance on clean speech, it significantly improves robustness in noisy and reverberant environments across FFT and PEFT settings. Our best-performing system, fully fine-tuned myMediWhisper-Medium without augmentation, achieves a state-of-the-art Word Error Rate (WER) of 23.44%, outperforming much larger general-domain fine-tuned models. Dataset and other resources can be found at the Huggingface repository: https://huggingface.co/datasets/LULab/mediTalk-mm-rdy.

</details>


### [169] [TEAMMix: Taxonomy Enrichment Augmentation and Minority-augmented Mixing Strategy for LLM-enhanced Weak-Supervised Hierarchical Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11044)
*Jian Zhang,Zhuohao Yang,Songlin Lei,Bangli Liu,Ziwei Wang,Xufeng Weng,Gehan Amaratunga,Yu Lin,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM数据增强的弱监督层次文本分类框架，通过关键词生成与语料挖掘丰富标签层次语义，利用LLM生成伪样本缓解长尾问题，并采用高斯混合模型进行置信度重采样优化数据质量，实验表明该方法能有效提升LLM生成伪标签的可靠性及细粒度、不均衡数据集上的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次文本分类面临标签层次复杂和类别不均衡等挑战，现有基于大型语言模型的方法因提示词冗长和标签结构信息丢失等问题难以高效应用。

Method: 提出弱监督HTC框架，包含三个模块：1）通过关键词生成和语料挖掘丰富标签层次语义；2）引导LLM生成伪样本缓解长尾问题；3）采用高斯混合模型进行基于置信度的重采样来优化生成数据的质量。

Result: 实验证明该方法有效提升了LLM生成伪标签的可靠性，并在细粒度及不均衡数据集上显著增强了分类性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效解决了现有LLM方法在HTC任务中的不足，验证了数据增强与置信度重采样策略在弱监督层次文本分类中的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Hierarchical Text Classification (HTC), as a critical text mining task, faces challenges such as complex label hierarchies and class imbalance. Existing methods based on large language models (LLMs) struggle to be efficiently applied to this task due to issues like lengthy prompts and loss of label structural information. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a weakly supervised HTC framework enhanced by LLM-based data augmentation. The framework first enriches the label hierarchy semantically through keyword generation and corpus mining, thereby enhancing the model's understanding of labels. Subsequently, it guides the LLM to generate pseudo-samples to mitigate the long-tail problem, and employs a Gaussian mixture model for confidence-based resampling to optimize the quality of generated data. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively improves the reliability of LLM-generated pseudo-labels and significantly enhances classification performance on fine-grained and imbalanced datasets.

</details>


### [170] [Multiclass Sentiment Analysis for Identifying Political Viewpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11049)
*Girma Yohannis Bade,Olga Kolesnikova,Jose Luis Oropeza,Grigori Sidorov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探索社交媒体政治话语的多类别情感分析任务，设计了基于XGBoost和BERT两种机器学习方法，在政治社交媒体帖子数据集上进行训练和评估。实验结果显示XGBoost模型F1分数为0.2835，BERT模型为0.2806，表明政治话语情感分类具有较高挑战性，并为未来研究提供了基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体的快速发展产生了大量政治话语，为分析公众意见和识别不同政治观点提供了宝贵机会。多类别政治观点情感分析对于理解政治话语至关重要，但目前该任务具有较高的复杂性，需要探索有效的自动化分类方法。

Method: 本文设计了两种机器学习方法：基于XGBoost的方法和基于BERT的方法，用于对政治社交媒体帖子的多类别情感进行分类。模型在带标签的政治社交媒体帖子数据集上进行训练和评估，使用标准分类指标（F1分数）衡量性能。

Result: 实验结果显示，XGBoost模型在测试集上达到0.2835的F1分数，BERT模型达到0.2806的F1分数，两者性能相近但整体准确率偏低，反映了政治话语情感分类任务的复杂性。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了复杂且情境化的政治话语情感分类具有较大挑战性，为未来多类别政治情感分析研究提供了基线参考，同时也表明需要开发更有效的方法来应对该任务。

Abstract: The rapid growth of social media has created vast amounts of political discourse, which provides valuable opportunities to analyze public opinions and identify different political perspectives. Sentiment Analysis (SA) is a core task in Natural Language Processing (NLP) that allows the computational study of attitudes and opinions in textual data, and has become increasingly important for understanding political discourse. In this work, we investigate multiclass sentiment analysis of political view- points on social media, that is to automatically discriminate multiple sentiment classes over political issues and figures. To solve this task we design and evaluate two machine-learning approaches based on XGBoost and BERT. We train and evaluate the models on a labeled dataset of political social media posts using standard classification metrics. The experimental results show that the XGBoost model reaches an F1-score of 0.2835 and the BERT- based model reaches an F1-score of 0.2806 on the test set. These results demonstrate the challenge of classifying complex and contextualized political discourse sentiment and provide a baseline for future research in multiclass political sentiment analysis.

</details>


### [171] [Actions Speak Louder than Words: Measuring Cross-Lingual Policy Retention in Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11110)
*Sourabrata Mukherjee,Kalika Bali,Sunayana Sitaram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将"动作策略"（而非最终答案）作为多语言工具型Agent评测的度量对象，跨越8个模型、6个并行基准和41种语言进行了238万次回放实验。研究发现五个混杂因素会使朴素的多语言动作相似度测量产生误导，逐一矫正后效应反而更大：语言间分歧是结构性的而非采样噪声，前沿模型在贪心解码下保留71-73%的动作策略，但小于10B参数的模型会崩溃，且模型倾向于将非英语任务路由到英语处理（这一行为因果关键且难以改变）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言评测通常只比较最终答案而丢弃动作轨迹，但动作才是系统的实际产物——决定了成本和延迟、失败模式，且是唯一可审计的行为。作者旨在填补这一空白，将动作策略本身作为被测量的对象。

Method: 在8个模型、6个并行基准、41种语言上进行2.38M次回放实验，测量动作轨迹相似度。系统性地识别并消除五个混杂因素（短轨迹得分偏高、空轨迹得满分、无关轨迹过半概率偶然一致、上限受模型可复现性约束、同语言重复提问无基线），并通过置换检验、贪心解码、温度扫描及预注册预测等方法验证结论稳健性。

Result: 每个混杂因素矫正后效应都变大：分歧是结构性的（贪心解码下仍存在、随温度升高保持平稳）；按自身可复现性归一化后，四个前沿模型在贪心解码下收敛，跨语言保留71-73%的动作策略，模型身份仅解释5.7%方差；小于约10B参数时崩溃，小模型排序多为偶然地板的人为产物；非英语任务被路由到英语处理，这一转向是因果关键的，且模型被告知后也不会放弃；一个轨迹提取正则表达式（而非模型本身）就能制造多语言失败——两个示例使某个模型测得准确率提升26倍，而可读输出准确率几乎不变。

Conclusion: 多语言动作策略一致性是可测量且结构性的：大模型跨语言保留大部分动作策略，且显著受"英语中枢"路由机制驱动；评测中的测量伪影（如轨迹提取正则）可能比模型本身更能制造"多语言失败"，评估方法论需对测量工具本身保持警惕。

Abstract: When a tool-using agent is given the same task in a different language, does it still take the same steps? Multilingual evaluation rarely asks: it compares final answers and discards the actions. Yet those actions are the product: they fix cost and latency, decide how the system fails, and are the only auditable part of its behaviour. We make the action policy the measured object across 8 models, 6 parallel benchmarks and 41 languages (2.38M rollouts). The naive measurement fails: five confounds sit between raw trace similarity and any defensible claim, each able to flip a conclusion. Short traces score higher, empty traces score perfectly, unrelated traces agree by chance over half the time, the gap is capped by each model's reproducibility, and a model asked the same question twice in one language answers differently, leaving no baseline. We remove all five, and every correction makes the effect larger. Divergence proves structural, not sampling noise: it survives greedy decoding in every cell and stays flat as temperature rises, even as models grow less self-consistent. Normalised by their own reproducibility, four very different frontier models converge under greedy decoding, each keeping 71-73% of its action policy across languages, with model identity explaining only 5.7% of the variance. Below roughly 10B parameters it breaks down, and the ordering among smaller models is largely an artifact of a chance floor we measure by permutation rather than assume. Agents route non-English tasks through English; this pivot is causally load-bearing, confirmed by a pre-registered prediction across four models, and models will not abandon it when told to. Finally, a single trace-extraction regex, not the model, manufactured a multilingual failure: two worked examples raise one model's measured accuracy twenty-sixfold while its accuracy on readable outputs barely moves.

</details>


### [172] [Attention-Path Fragility as an Uncertainty Signal in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11138)
*Minsoo Kim,Sungyoung Ji,Kisung Moon,Ilyong Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ASMI（注意力子网络互信息）作为一种免训练的估计器，通过掩码注意力头并测量子网络之间的BALD互信息来捕捉模型预测的"脆弱性"。该方法在基于语境的问答任务中能提供超越单次置信度和熵的误差预测信息，尤其在"自信但脆弱"的预测上效果显著，且在12个设置中有10个达到或超过最强基线Semantic Entropy。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性估计方法主要依赖输出分布的广度（如置信度、熵），但忽略了预测在扰动下是否保持稳定。作者认为，一个自信的预测可能是"脆弱"的——即对注意力通路微扰高度敏感，这种脆弱性本身携带独立的误差预测信号。

Method: 提出ASMI：通过掩码不同注意力头生成子网络，测量子网络之间输出的BALD互信息，并用语义一致性核函数来折扣表面形式分歧。还提出Sem-ASMI变体，可从单次贪心解码中读取信号而无需随机采样。

Result: 在基于语境的问答中，ASMI在12个基准-骨干组合中有10个达到或超过Semantic Entropy；最佳变体（自适应版本）在8个设置中持平或领先最强基线，其中3个在配对检验下显著。在参数化QA中所有变体退化到MSP基线水平，与预测一致。

Conclusion: ASMI的独特信号不是对输出置信度的简单重述，其适用性具有领域分级特性：在答案通过提供上下文路由时有效，在参数化知识回忆时受限。模型自身能预测该方法何时适用，其边界由注意力头层面脆弱性与误差的耦合程度决定。

Abstract: We propose that a model's uncertainty about a token is reflected not only in the breadth of its output distribution but also in whether a confident prediction is \emph{fragile} under perturbation of its attention pathways. We instantiate this as ASMI (Attention-Subnetwork Mutual Information), a training-free estimator that masks attention heads and measures the BALD mutual information among the resulting subnetworks, with a semantic-agreement kernel to discount surface-form disagreement. The signal is not a restatement of output confidence: on grounded QA an out-of-fold test shows it adds error-predictive information beyond single-pass confidence and entropy, concentrated in \emph{confident-but-fragile} predictions, where acting on it roughly halves the retained error of a confidence filter. The distinctness is regime-graded, so ASMI predicts its own domain of applicability, strong where answers are routed through provided context and bounded by design where they are recalled from parametric knowledge. Sem-ASMI reads the signal from a single greedy response, without the stochastic generations the strongest baselines require, and ties or beats Semantic Entropy on ten of the twelve grounded benchmark-backbone settings. Across the same twelve settings, the best ASMI variant, typically the adaptive one reusing the ten samples already drawn for the baselines, ties or leads the strongest baseline in eight, significantly in three under a paired test. On parametric QA all variants revert to or below the zero-cost MSP baseline, exactly as predicted, and the estimates are near-deterministic across reruns. A head-level analysis shows that what tracks this boundary is not the presence of head-level fragility but whether that fragility couples to errors.

</details>


### [173] [The Illusion of Cross-Lingual Safety in Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11146)
*Abigail Oppong,P Sam Sahil,Tadesse Destaw Belay,Maryam Ibrahim Mukhtar,Esmael Ahmed Abdu,Tassallah Abdullahi,Jessica Oparebea,Saminu Mohammad Aliyu,Idris Abdulmumin,Abubakar Juma Chilala,Nicholaus Dismas Ladislaus,Alfred Malengo Kondoro,Lemofouet Valdini Douglace,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,Seid Muhie Yimam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）在多语言环境下的安全对齐问题，聚焦四种非洲低资源语言（Twi、豪萨语、阿姆哈拉语和斯瓦希里语），提出了新数据集LoDNA和潜在几何框架LDN。实验发现跨语言安全迁移严重受限，有害提示仅保留不到10%的英语拒绝信号，表明当前多语言安全对齐是表面性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的安全对齐主要在英语中开发，假设其能跨语言泛化，但这一假设鲜少被验证。低资源语言的安全漏洞可能被恶意利用，亟需系统的跨语言安全迁移评估。

Method: 构建LoDNA安全数据集（包含逐字翻译和文化本地化提示），提出潜在几何框架，通过探测隐藏状态的拒绝表示来评估跨语言安全转移，并分析语义对齐与层间漂移。

Result: 跨语言安全迁移严重不足：大多数语言-模型组合中有害提示保留的英语拒绝信号不足10%；逐字与本地化提示虽语义高度对齐（余弦相似度0.95-0.996），但表示在层间漂移，表明模型编码了概念却未将其路由到安全机制。

Conclusion: 当前多语言安全对齐是表面性的，研究结果强烈反驳了语言无关的通用危害流形假设，至少在所研究的低资源语言中不成立。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) is largely developed in English, assuming these safeguards generalize across multilingual settings. However, this assumption remains underexplored and exposes a vulnerability in low-resource languages. We investigate cross-lingual safety transfer in four African languages, Twi, Hausa, Amharic, and Swahili, using LoDNA, a new safety dataset that pairs literal translations with culturally localized prompts. To move beyond generation-based evaluation, we propose a latent geometric framework that probes hidden-state refusal representations in LLMs. Our experimental results show that cross-lingual safety transfer is severely limited; harmful prompts retain less than 10% of the English refusal signal across most language-model pairs. Literal and localized prompts are semantically aligned (cosine 0.95-0.996) but drift across layers, suggesting models encode the concepts without routing them to safety mechanisms. These findings demonstrate that current multilingual safety alignment is superficial, providing strong evidence against the assumption of a universal, language-agnostic harm manifold within the specific low-resource languages studied. Warning: This paper contains example data that may be offensive or harmful.

</details>


### [174] [From Interpretability to Control: Insights from Six Years of the TrustNLP Workshop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11171)
*Rahul Gupta,Abhinav Mohanty,Anaelia Ovalle,Anil Ramakrishna,Anubrata Das,Apurv Verma,Jwala Dhamala,Ninareh Mehrabi,Tharindu Kumarage,Yada Pruksachatkun,Yang Trista Cao,Kai-Wei Chang,Aram Galstyan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对TrustNLP研讨会（2021-2026共六届）144篇论文进行了系统性综述，揭示了NLP领域从"事后解释静态模型"向"机制理解与主动控制生成系统"的转变，并对六个信任维度进行了分类分析，同时与ACL等四大主会约2000篇论文进行了跨会议对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的能力涌现和广泛部署，可信NLP研究快速增长但缺乏系统性梳理。作者希望通过综合六届TrustNLP研讨会论文，识别信任研究的演化趋势、维度分布及其与主会研究方向的关联，为社区提供结构化的方向指引。

Method: 作者汇总了TrustNLP研讨会2021至2026年间全部144篇论文，基于TrustLLM和DecodingTrust等已有框架，将其分类至六个信任维度。此外，将TrustNLP的论文主题分布与同期ACL、NAACL、EACL、EMNLP约2000篇论文进行了跨会议比较分析。

Result: 研究发现：首个高影响力聊天模型的发布同时激活了所有信任维度，后续模型世代将焦点转向真实性和安全对齐；真实性是增长最快的维度（2021-2022年缺席，到2025-2026年占37%）；公平性是最稳定的主题；可解释性呈现U形轨迹（事后方法衰退后，机制可解释性于2026年使其复兴）。TrustNLP的主题分布与领域平均水平高度一致。

Conclusion: 作者识别出四个结构性洞见，并为研究社区提出了可操作的方向建议，强调可信NLP研究正从被动解读向主动控制生成系统演化。

Abstract: The Workshop on Trustworthy Natural Language Processing (TrustNLP), co-located with major ACL conferences since 2021, has grown from 8 proceedings papers to 41 over six editions, documenting a field-wide transition from post-hoc interpretability of static models to mechanistic understanding and proactive control of generative systems. We synthesize insights from all 144 proceedings papers, classifying them along six trust dimensions grounded in established frameworks (TrustLLM, DecodingTrust). We observe co-occurrences with capability emergence. The release of the first high-impact chat models activated all trust dimensions simultaneously, while subsequent model generations shifted focus toward truthfulness and safety alignment. Analysis from the classification study reveals that truthfulness is the fastest-growing dimension (absent in 2021-2022, comprising 37% of papers by 2025-2026), fairness remains the most consistent theme, and explainability exhibits a U-shaped trajectory; declining as post-hoc methods lost relevance but resurging in 2026 through mechanistic interpretability. A cross-venue comparison with ACL, NAACL, EACL, and EMNLP (~2K papers) in the same period shows that TrustNLP's topical distribution closely follows the field average. We identify four structural insights and conclude with actionable directions for the research community.

</details>


### [175] [ConVAWG: A Retrieval-Grounded Framework for Controlled Synthetic Dialogue Generation in Violence Against Women and Girls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11200)
*Chen Lyu,Xingwei Tan,Simon Cullen,Shelley Wilson,Lois Arthurs,Arshad Jhumka,Gabriele Pergola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ConVAWG框架，用于生成符合CPS（儿童保护服务）对齐的合成暴力侵害女性与女孩（VAWG）多轮对话数据集，基于检索增强的方式构建场景、事件时间线和多场景角色扮演对话，并释放了包含200个场景、6000多个多轮对话事件的标注数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实对话数据因隐私和法律约束难以获取、发布和标注，尤其在家庭暴力等敏感领域；现有工作仅关注句子级在线辱骂毒性，缺少将虐待建模为关系性和时间展开现象的对话级研究。

Method: ConVAWG通过从人物种子、英国国家统计局的人口统计模式、官方犯罪定义和检索到的国内凶杀案审查案例构建场景；将场景转化为分层事件时间线；生成多场景角色扮演对话；并对适当话语施加针对性的激活引导毒性控制。

Result: 释放了200个场景、6000多个多轮对话事件，附带丰富的场景级、事件级和轮次级元数据；通过广泛的人工评估、LLM作为评判者的评估、消融研究和下游任务验证，显示出较强的对话质量和领域保真度。

Conclusion: ConVAWG证明检索增强的合成数据生成是填补敏感领域对话数据空白、支持VAWG相关关系性与时间性虐待建模的有效途径。

Abstract: Synthetic dialogue generation offers a way to study conversational dynamics in sensitive domains where real data are difficult to access, release, or annotate. The underlying abuse may occur online or offline: threats and coercion can appear directly in messages, while behaviours such as surveillance, isolation, stalking, and physical violence may be planned, disclosed, or referred to conversationally. Privacy and legal constraints make it difficult the release of large-scale real conversation datasets; existing work has mostly focused on sentence-level toxicity of online abuses, leaving a gap in modelling abuse as a relational and temporally unfolding phenomenon. In this work, we focus on modelling Violence Against Women and Girls (VAWG) scenarios as multi-turn dialogues. We introduce ConVAWG, a retrieval-grounded framework for generating CPS-aligned synthetic VAWG chat dialogues. ConVAWG builds scenarios from persona seeds, demographic patterns reported by the UK Office for National Statistics, official crime definitions, and retrieved Domestic Homicide Review cases; converts them into hierarchical event timelines; generates multi-scene role-play dialogues; and applies targeted activation-steered toxicity control to appropriate utterances. We release over 6,000 multi-turn dialogue events across 200 scenarios with rich scenario-, event-, and turn-level metadata. Extensive human evaluation, LLM-as-Judge assessment, ablations, and downstream tasks show strong dialogue quality and domain fidelity.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Beyond Cash Flows: A Multi-Agent AI Framework for Valuing Clinical-Stage, Cross-Border Biotechnology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10175)
*Yuhan Fang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个专为临床阶段生物技术公司设计的专业多智能体投资分析框架，解决传统现金流估值范式在该领域的失效问题。框架包含估值层、跨市场协调层和冲突融合机制三个核心组件，其方法论源于作者作为中国首只跨境生物技术基金唯一的投资组合管理人的人工实践（16个月回报127.17%，基准50.67%），但本文仅呈现架构层面的设计原则，未评估任何AI系统实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型多智能体投资框架依赖于传统现金流估值假设，无法适用于临床阶段生物技术领域——该领域的企业价值完全取决于二元科学和监管里程碑事件，需要专门的框架来弥合这一范式差距。

Method: 提出专业多智能体框架，包含三个核心层：1）估值层——将定性科学判断转化为可辩护的无收入资产估值；2）跨市场协调层——在国际交易所同时协调定价；3）冲突融合机制——以领域特定方式系统性地仲裁乐观科学信念与谨慎监管约束之间的冲突。该架构编码了作者作为基金经理手工执行的真实方法，而非推测性设计。

Result: 本文未评估任何AI系统实现，仅以架构层面的形式呈现框架。其方法依据是作者人工执行该方法时的真实业绩记录：16个月内实现127.17%的回报，对比基准50.67%。该记录是底层方法的证据，而非任何AI系统的证据。

Conclusion: 该框架为将智能体投资系统扩展到它们当前服务不足的、复杂的、事件驱动的资产类别建立了基础性设计原则。作者的人工投资记录为底层方法提供了实证支持，为未来实现和评估该多智能体框架奠定了基础。

Abstract: A new class of software systems is transforming investment analysis. Large language model agents assembled into collaborative team structures including analysts, researchers, and risk managers are increasingly deployed across financial markets. Yet current multi-agent frameworks share a critical limitation: they rely on the foundational assumption that companies can be valued through traditional cash flows. This paradigm fails in clinical-stage biotechnology, where enterprise value depends entirely on binary scientific and regulatory milestones. To bridge this gap, this paper introduces a specialized multi-agent framework. Its valuation layer translates qualitative scientific judgment into defensible valuations for pre-revenue assets; its cross-market coordination layer reconciles pricing across international venues simultaneously; and its conflict-fusion mechanism systematically arbitrates between bullish scientific conviction and cautious regulatory constraints in a domain-specific manner. Crucially, the architecture is not a speculative design: it encodes a method the author first executed by hand as sole portfolio manager of China's first dedicated cross-border biotechnology fund, a human practice that returned 127.17% against a 50.67% benchmark within sixteen months. That record is evidence for the underlying method rather than for any AI system; no implementation is evaluated here. This paper presents the framework at the architectural level, establishing foundational design principles for extending agentic investment systems into complex, event-driven asset classes they currently serve poorly.

</details>


### [177] [The Deliberative Deficit: An Empirical Critique of LLMs in Democratic Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10186)
*Maurice Flechtner*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文通过应用政治学中的审议理性指数（DRI），对11个前沿模型配置在12个公民大会议题上的1,980组（每组5个智能体）LLM讨论进行分析，发现LLM群组的话语程序质量接近人类，但视角多样性仅约为人类的三分之一，且与人类审议（多样性收敛）相反，LLM审议反而增加观点分散度，因此LLM适合作为支持人类推理的工具，而非自主审议主体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被越来越多地部署在需要集体推理的复杂价值问题上，但现有基准主要关注可验证任务（数学、编码、协调博弈），无法有效评估无客观正确答案的多元主义问题上的推理能力，且程序性评估（尊重性、论证、参与度）被证明系统性不足。

Method: 将政治学中开发并经过公民大会验证的审议理性指数（DRI）应用于LLM群组推理评估，综合分析了1,980组五智能体LLM在12个公民大会议题上的讨论，覆盖11个前沿模型配置，并与人类审议进行对比。

Result: LLM群组的话语程序质量与人类审议相当，但主体间一致性提升幅度小且依赖于议题（集中在可处理问题而非伦理争议问题）。LLM群组的视角多样性约为人类的三分之一，且审议方向与人类相反：人类审议减少观点分散度，LLM审议反而增加分散度。通过人物角色提示工程增加多样性无法恢复人类动态，反而反转了审议推理中更新的组成部分。

Conclusion: 结论是"约束而非禁止"：LLM可以作为支持人类在多元主义问题上推理的工具，但当前证据不允许将其视为自主审议主体。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed in settings that require collective reasoning on complex, value-laden problems. Confidence in these deployments rests largely on benchmarks for verifiable tasks (mathematics, coding, coordination games), yet many of these applications concern problems where no objectively correct answer exists and where decision quality instead depends on integrating pluralistic perspectives to find mutually acceptable solutions. We argue that LLM reasoning capacity on this class of problems cannot be fully inferred from verifiable-task benchmarks, and that procedural evaluations of LLM discourse (respectfulness, justification, engagement) are systematically insufficient. We apply the Deliberative Reason Index (DRI), a measure developed in political science and validated across citizen assemblies, as a tool for evaluating reliable group-level reasoning on pluralistic, non-verifiable problems. Synthesizing recent evidence across 1,980 five-agent LLM runs on 12 citizen-assembly topics across 11 frontier model configurations, we find that LLM groups produce discourse with procedural quality comparable to human deliberation, while gains in intersubjective consistency are small, topic-dependent, and concentrated on tractable rather than ethically contested questions. LLM groups exhibit roughly one-third the perspective diversity of human assemblies and reverse the human convergence pattern: human deliberation decreases dispersion as diverse views synthesise, whereas LLM deliberation increases it. Engineering diversity through persona prompting does not restore the human dynamic but inverts which component of deliberative reasoning is updated. Our conclusion is constraining rather than prohibitive: LLMs can function as tools supporting human reasoning on pluralistic problems, but current evidence does not license treating them as autonomous deliberative agents.

</details>


### [178] [Not a Monolith: Lab-Level Divergence in the Cooperative Equilibria of Chinese Frontier LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10262)
*Francisco León Zúñiga Bolívar*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究考察了四个前沿中国大模型（DeepSeek V4 Pro、Qwen3-Max、Kimi K2.5、GLM-5.1）在进化囚徒困境中的合作倾向，通过固定代码转换器消除混淆变量，发现中国模型并非铁板一块：四个实验室在攻击性均衡比例上存在显著差异（1%-9%），生态系统内差异超过东西方差距。合作偏向的普遍性假设得到有保留的支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究仅记录了西方前沿LLM智能体的合作偏向，但中国模型（不同对齐谱系）是否共享该偏向尚不清楚；同时，将"中国模型"视为单一整体是否合理也需要实证检验。

Method: 在进化迭代囚徒困境中测试四个中国前沿模型，使用固定的GPT-5.4 Mini作为代码转换器（消除策略倾向与编码能力的混淆），运行全对决锦标赛和Moran过程，每种条件n=500次，覆盖三种提示风格和四种种群制度，对两个预注册假设进行检验。

Result: H6（非单一整体）得到支持：四个实验室在攻击性均衡比例上显著不同（Qwen3-Max为1%，DeepSeek V4 Pro为9%），六对两两比较中有四对通过Holm-Bonferroni校正；组内差异（8个百分点）大于东西方生态系统的均值差。H5（合作偏向普遍性）得到有条件支持：12个实验室-提示组合中有6个呈现合作多数，而西方模型为9/12，但在备选转换器下该比例升至9/12。

Conclusion: 合作倾向设置的单元是实验室而非生态系统，将"中国模型"视为铁板一块缺乏证据支持；合作偏向的普遍性尚需进一步验证。

Abstract: Does the cooperative bias documented for Western frontier LLM agents extend to a different alignment lineage, and should the Chinese models that embody it be treated as a single bloc or as distinct laboratories? We study four frontier-tier Chinese models - DeepSeek V4 Pro, Qwen3-Max, Kimi K2.5 and GLM-5.1 - in an evolutionary Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma, under a design that removes a confound present in prior work. Rather than letting each model convert its own natural-language strategies into code, which entangles strategic disposition with coding ability, we hold the converter fixed (GPT-5.4 Mini) across all labs, so every cross-lab comparison is a comparison of generation alone. We run the full protocol: all-play-all tournaments and a Moran process at n=500 runs per condition, across three prompt styles and four population regimes. Two pre-registered hypotheses are evaluated. H6 (not monolithic) is supported: the four labs differ significantly in aggressive-equilibrium proportion, P_A running from 1% for Qwen3-Max to 9% for DeepSeek V4 Pro, with four of six pairwise comparisons surviving Holm-Bonferroni. The spread across the four labs (P_A range 8pp) is larger than the difference between the Chinese and Western ecosystems' mean P_A (5.0% vs 5.0%): on this measure, within-ecosystem variation exceeds the East-West gap. H5 (cooperative-bias generality) is consistent but qualified: a cooperative plurality holds in 6 of 12 lab-prompt combinations against the 9 of 12 reported for Western models, a difference we do not treat as firm, since the count rests on Cooperative-Neutral near-ties and rises to 9/12 under an alternate converter in our pre-registered robustness check. The lab, not the ecosystem, is the unit at which cooperative disposition is set; treating "Chinese models" as a monolith is not supported by the evidence.

</details>


### [179] [ASCon: A Direction-Aware Reciprocal Agent--Step Contextualization Model for Failure Attribution in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10646)
*Shuyu Jiang,Yue Ran,Kaiyu Xu,Xingshu Chen,Yi Zhang,Hao Ren,Rui Tang,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出ASCon，一个面向LLM多智能体系统中多种故障归因目标的统一表示模型，通过方向感知的智能体-步骤互语境化建模，在不同归因任务上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统故障归因方法多针对单一归因目标开发专用模型，忽视了不同归因目标（故障智能体、故障步骤、故障模式）之间的证据依赖关系。然而这些目标虽不同，却依赖来自MAS轨迹的相同诊断证据（任务约束、智能体角色、行为历史、智能体间交互），这促使作者探索统一表示模型。

Method: 提出ASCon（方向感知的智能体-步骤互语境化模型），包含三个核心组件：1) 方向感知图注意力建模执行上下文；2) 掩蔽的步骤到智能体注意力构建行为感知的智能体表示；3) 智能体条件化的步骤语境化将智能体上下文融合回步骤表示。最终通过轻量级任务特定头部适配不同归因目标。

Result: 实验表明：故障智能体检测微准确率提升5.83%以上，故障步骤检测微准确率提升10.63%以上，故障模式检测Macro-F1提升14.73%以上。同时，在域外场景中显著增强基于LLM方法的归因能力。

Conclusion: ASCon通过统一的轨迹证据表示和方向感知的互语境化建模，有效解决了多智能体系统中多个故障归因目标的一致性建模问题，并能在域外场景中增强LLM方法的归因能力。

Abstract: Failure attribution in LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) aims to answer who caused failures, when they occurred, and why by identifying responsible targets including faulty agents, erroneous steps, and failure modes. Existing methods have primarily focused on developing dedicated models for specific attribution targets, with limited attention to the evidential dependencies among them. Despite these attribution targets are different, they rely on common diagnostic evidence from MAS trajectories, including task constraints, agent roles, behavioral histories and inter-agent interactions. This commonality motivates us to develop a unified representation model that aggregates the trajectory evidence into individual agent and step representations, which can subsequently be adapted to different attribution targets. Accordingly, we propose ASCon, a direction-aware reciprocal \textbf{A}gent--\textbf{S}tep \textbf{Con}textualization model for multiple failure attribution targets. ASCon introduces direction-aware graph attention to model execution context, masked step-to-agent attention to construct behavior-aware agent representations, and agent-conditioned step contextualization to incorporate agent context back into step representations. The resulting contextualized representations enable different attribution targets through lightweight target-specific heads. Experiments show that ASCon can improve faulty-agent detection by 5.83\%+ in micro-accuracy, faulty-step detection by 10.63\%+ in micro-accuracy, and failure-mode detection by 14.73\%+ in Macro-F1. Meanwhile, it can also substantially enhance the LLM-based methods' attribution capabilities in out-of-domain scenarios.

</details>


### [180] [Reifying Research Logic: AI-Assisted Workflow Construction and Incremental Refinement for Quantitative Syntax](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10662)
*He Wang,Jingbo Chen,Yuqiao Lai,Nan Yang,Hanwen Zhang,Wei Yuan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出QLWF，一个将自然语言研究描述转化为可执行工作流的可视化平台，通过AI辅助的五阶段流水线实现。在64任务的QL-Bench基准上，所有任务均生成结构有效且可执行的工作流，平均输出合理性达98.4%，且支持增量精修以节省约三分之二的token开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量语言研究依赖长链计算步骤，但连接这些步骤的逻辑通常隐藏在脚本中，导致分析难以检查、共享和修订。作者希望让研究逻辑可见化、可执行且可复用。

Method: QLWF采用五阶段流水线，将自然语言研究描述转化为可执行工作流。具体而言，通过物化（reification）使研究逻辑以工作流形式可见，通过形式化（formalization）赋予工作流确定性执行语义。语言模型仅用于构建阶段，执行由固定节点库和引擎处理以确保可复现性。平台还支持增量精修，允许仅修改需要变更的部分。

Result: 构建了包含64个任务的QL-Bench基准，所有任务均生成结构有效且可执行的工作流，平均输出合理性达98.4%，远超基于提示的基线。在12任务生命周期基准上，精修过程全部成功，且仅需全量重新生成约三分之一的token。同时发布了节点库、基准、工作流模板和平台资源。

Conclusion: QLWF验证了将AI辅助工作流构建应用于定量句法研究的可行性，在可复现性、可检查性和修订效率方面均有显著优势，为定量语言学研究提供了可复用的基础设施资源。

Abstract: Quantitative language research often depends on long chains of computational steps, yet the logic connecting those steps usually remains buried in scripts. This makes analyses harder to inspect, share, and revise than they need to be. Focusing on quantitative syntax, we present QLWF, a visual workflow platform that turns natural-language research descriptions into executable workflows through an AI assisted five-stage pipeline. In this setting, reification makes the research logic visible as a workflow, while formalization gives that workflow deterministic execution semantics. The language model is used only during construction. Execution is handled by a fixed node library and engine, which keeps the resulting workflows reproducible. QLWF also supports incremental refinement, so saved workflows can be revised by changing only the parts that need to change rather than being rebuilt from scratch. To evaluate the approach, we build a 64-task benchmark called QL-Bench from the quantitative-syntax literature. Across three runs, QLWF produces structurally valid and executable workflows for every task and reaches a mean output-plausibility rate of 98.4%, well above the prompt-based baselines. On a separate 12-task lifecycle benchmark, this refinement process succeeds in every case and uses roughly one-third of the tokens required by full regeneration. The paper also releases the node library, benchmark, workflow templates, and platform as reusable resources for quantitative-syntax research.

</details>


### [181] [Who Are You Explaining To? A Multi-Agent System for Audience-Aware XAI Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11033)
*Francesco Musicco,Danilo Danese,Giuseppe Fasano,Angela Lombardi,Alberto Carlo Maria Mancino,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了XstrAI，一个受众感知的多智能体框架，用于将SHAP等局部特征归因解释转化为适合不同受众（患者、临床医生、数据科学家）的自然语言叙述。该框架将局部解释编码为不变的、跨受众一致的结构化表示，并通过三个专门化的LLM智能体（受众规划、语言实现、验证）进行生成，带有有界修订循环。在糖尿病和中风风险预测任务上，XstrAI在忠实性和受众适切性上优于11个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHAP等特征归因方法提供的数值输出对不同专业背景、目标和误解读风险的受众（如患者、临床医生、数据科学家）很少足够。在医疗AI中，同一个局部解释需要通过不同的沟通形式传达给不同受众，而直接通过LLM进行朴素文本化容易导致证据锚定不足、将归因与因果语言混淆，以及生成有说服力但未忠实反映模型证据的内容。

Method: XstrAI将每个预测案例编码为不可变的结构化表示，跨受众共享相同的底层证据。生成过程分为三个专门化的LLM智能体：(1)受众感知规划，(2)语言实现，(3)验证（检查词句锚定、归因一致性、沟通风险和受众适切性），并带有有界修订循环以纠正检测到的不一致。评估包含叙述内部忠诚度（对SHAP证据的保真度）和叙述外部适切性（参考语料库、多家族LLM评审、目标读者调查）两个维度，对比11个基线。

Result: 在糖尿病和中风风险预测任务上，XstrAI生成的叙述被独立评审者一致地分配到其目标受众，并且在临床医生和患者受众上优于所有基线。在数据科学家受众上与受众条件化的单提示基线相比表现具有竞争力（略逊一筹）。

Conclusion: XstrAI证明了将局部解释视为固定证据、并系统化其沟通结构的多智能体方法，能够生成既忠实于模型证据又适合不同受众需求的解释性叙述，是医疗AI可解释性沟通的一种有效范式。

Abstract: Feature-attribution methods such as SHAP provide useful evidence about individual model predictions, but their numerical outputs are rarely sufficient for audiences with different expertise, goals, and risks of misinterpretation. In medical AI, the same local explanation must reach patients, clinicians, and data scientists through markedly different forms of communication, and naive verbalization through large language models (LLMs) is prone to weak grounding, conflation of attribution with causal language, and outputs that are persuasive without being faithful to the underlying model evidence. We introduce XstrAI, an audience-aware multi-agent framework that treats local explanations as fixed evidence and structures how it is communicated to each target reader. Each prediction case is encoded as an immutable structured representation, shared identically across audiences so the underlying evidence remains fixed. Generation is factored into three specialized LLM agents responsible for audience-aware planning, linguistic realization, and validation for grounding, attribution consistency, communicative risk, and audience appropriateness, with a bounded revision loop triggered on detected inconsistencies. We evaluate XstrAI on diabetes and stroke risk prediction against 11 baselines, ranging from direct verbalization to a re-implementation of a state-of-the-art narrator. The evaluation combines an intra-narrative regime measuring fidelity to SHAP evidence with an extra-narrative regime assessing audience appropriateness through reference corpora, multi-family LLM judges, and a survey with target readers. In both evaluations, XstrAI's narratives are consistently assigned to their intended audience by independent judges, and preferred over all baselines on Clinician and Patient audiences, with competitive performance on Data Scientist, where audience-conditioned single-prompt baselines lead.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [N2NMatcher: Towards Inlining-Resilient Binary Decomposition and Module Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10043)
*Ang Jia,He Jiang,Zhipeng Yang,Zhilei Ren,Xiaochen Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出N2NMatcher框架，用于解决函数内联导致的二进制代码相似性分析（BCSA）性能下降问题。该框架通过层次图神经网络预测稳定"锚点"函数，执行锚点边界分解和模块图嵌入匹配，从而提升分解质量和匹配精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BCSA方法按函数调用图（FCG）结构对二进制进行模块分解和匹配，但函数内联会改变FCG结构及函数语义，导致现有分解与匹配方法效果不佳。作者希望通过识别编译设置下保持稳定的函数边界来解决这一挑战。

Method: 首先进行实证研究，考察二进制中是否存在跨编译设置提供稳定模块边界的函数；然后利用层次图神经网络（对ACFG-FCG进行编码，融合操作码序列、控制流结构和FCG调用上下文）预测这类"锚点"函数；最后执行锚点边界分解，并利用学习到的模块图嵌入进行模块匹配。

Result: 实验结果表明，与现有方法相比，N2NMatcher在分解质量和模块匹配精度两方面均取得了提升，从而实现了更有效的程序级BCSA。

Conclusion: 通过将"锚点函数"预测与锚点边界分解相结合，N2NMatcher有效缓解了函数内联对二进制模块分解和匹配的负面影响，为程序级二进制代码相似性分析提供了更具鲁棒性的解决方案。

Abstract: Program-level Binary Code Similarity Analysis (BCSA) aims to identify semantically similar code regions across binary programs, serving as a fundamental technique for software plagiarism detection, vulnerability search, and malware analysis. Existing approaches often decompose binaries into modules following the structure of function call graphs (FCGs) and then match these modules by their contained functions. However, function inlining changes both FCG structures and binary function semantics, making existing decomposition and module matching methods less effective.
  In this work, we propose N2NMatcher, an inlining-resilient framework for binary decomposition and module matching. We first conduct an empirical study to examine whether binaries still contain functions that provide stable module boundaries across compilation settings. N2NMatcher learns to predict such functions as anchors using a hierarchical graph neural network that encodes binary ACFG-FCG representations built from opcode sequences, control-flow structures, and FCG calling context. It then performs anchor-bounded decomposition and matches the generated modules using learned module graph embeddings. Experimental results show that N2NMatcher improves both the decomposition quality and module matching accuracy compared to existing works, enabling more effective program-level BCSA.

</details>


### [183] [Exploring Semantic Stability Across Reviews in the Linux Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10101)
*Lucas Ciziks,Paulo Meirelles,Marco Aurélio Gerosa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了Linux IIO子系统代码审查过程中函数的演化轨迹，追踪了10,117条函数轨迹，发现75.3%的函数在版本间从未被修改，而在真正被编辑的轨迹中语义保持度高（相似度0.990 vs 基线0.909）。漂移主要集中在前几轮审查，且控制变量后仍存在统计上可检测但较小的残余效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于代码审查的研究通常只分析最终合并的补丁，而没有将其与首次提交版本进行比较，无法准确评估审查过程对代码的实际改变程度。

Method: 提出函数级测量方法，追踪Linux IIO子系统补丁系列中每个函数在版本间的轨迹（共10,117条），使用相似度评分与无关函数对基线进行比较，并区分被修改与未被修改的函数轨迹。

Result: 75.3%的轨迹从未被文本修改（造成100%相似度的假象）；仅看被编辑的轨迹，平均相似度为0.990（基线0.909），语义保留度高。审查漂移集中在第一轮，后续轮次主要因为更多未修改函数而显得保守。控制变量后仍有统计显著但较小的残余效应。

Conclusion: 代码审查中函数语义在很大程度上被保留，接近天花板的高相似度可能反映两种可能：审查确实保留了函数目的，或现有测量工具无法检测小而局部的编辑的重要性。该工作作为初步探索，需进一步研究。

Abstract: Code review is credited with substantially changing a patch's code between its first submission and the version that eventually lands. However, prior work typically studied only the final merged patch without comparing it to the first submission. We present a function-level measurement that tracks 10,117 trajectories (each function followed across the numbered revisions of one patch series) through the patch history of the Linux IIO subsystem, comparing similarity scores against unrelated function pairs as a baseline. A naive reading yields near-total similarity, but this is largely an artifact of composition: 75.3% of tracked trajectories are never textually modified between versions, contributing a trivial 100% similarity that inflates the headline. Restricting to the trajectories with a real edit, semantic purpose is still largely preserved (mean similarity 0.990 vs. a 0.909 baseline), but drift appears to concentrate in the first review round mainly because later rounds contain more functions that nobody touched, not because edits become more conservative over time. After controlling for it, a statistically detectable but small residual effect remains. This points to an open question: whether near-ceiling similarity reflects preserved purpose or a measurement tool that cannot detect the significance of small, localized edits. We present this work as a first look and outline next steps.

</details>


### [184] [One Recipe, Many Harnesses: What Self-Evolution Encodes Across Languages and Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10178)
*Siqi Yang,Qianlan Yang,Yu-Xiong Wang,Saurabh Pujar,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过固定演化配方，在8种编程语言和3个基础模型上系统分析自演化编程Agent（Self-evolving harness），发现演化产物实质上是"可读的补偿层"——弥补模型行为缺陷与语言工程需求之间的差距，而非针对特定基准的暗箱调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于自演化编程Agent的研究仅报告聚合性能提升，未深入分析演化产生的产物（prompts、工具、记忆）究竟编码了什么内容——是基准特定适配、语言工程知识、还是对底层模型局限的补偿？这种不透明性阻碍了对演化机制的理解和可解释性。

Method: 在Multi-SWE-Bench基准上固定演化配方，横跨8种编程语言和3个基础模型进行网格实验。每个编辑都通过类型化失败信号路由并记录为可证伪契约，使每次修改在演化后均可归因。系统性地解耦基准适配、语言知识和模型补偿三个因素。

Result: 四大发现：(1)演化循环在大多数单元中超越最小种子和手动设计的mini-SWE-agent基线，但存在两个零效果区域；(2)收益补偿可恢复的执行缺陷，缺陷密度接近零时收益也接近零，主导缺陷因单元而异；(3)演化产物跨语言共享抽象剧本，但以几乎不重叠的语言生态机制实例化；(4)共享核心可蒸馏为通用harness，而生态边际部分需原生重演化。

Conclusion: 自演化harness应被重新理解为可读的补偿层，由语言的工程需求和模型的行为差距共同塑造，而非不透明的基准调优脚手架。共享核心可提取并转移，但生态特定部分必须就地演化，这为设计和部署可解释的自演化系统提供了指导。

Abstract: Self-evolving harnesses are closed-loop systems in which an agent inspects its own rollouts and edits its prompts, tools, and memory. They reliably improve coding agents in evaluations, but prior work reports aggregate gains rather than analyzing what the evolved artifacts encode. It therefore remains unclear whether they encode benchmark-specific adaptations, language-specific engineering knowledge, or compensation for limitations of the underlying model. We disentangle these factors by holding an evolution recipe fixed across a grid of eight programming languages (Multi-SWE-Bench) and three base models, and analyzing the resulting harnesses. The recipe routes every edit through a typed failure signal and records it as a falsifiable contract, making each modification attributable after evolution. Four findings emerge. (1)The loop improves held-out solve rates over both a minimal seed and the manually designed mini-SWE-agent scaffold in most cells, but with two null regions. (2)Gains compensate recoverable execution defects, where defect mass is near zero, and gain is near zero; which defect dominates is cell-specific. A harness closes the gap between what a policy can do and what it does. (3)Evolved harnesses share an abstract playbook across languages but instantiate it with almost disjoint language ecosystem machinery. (4)The shared core transfers and can be distilled into one universal harness, while an ecosystem margin resists both and requires native re-evolution. Together, these results recast the evolved harness as a legible compensation layer, shaped jointly by the language's engineering demands and the model's behavioral gaps, rather than an opaque benchmark-tuned scaffold.

</details>


### [185] [Bias Smells in AI Software Development: Recognizing Potential Sources of Fairness Debt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10248)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Cleyton Magalhaes,Rodrigo Spinola*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过四个人工智能项目的探索性案例研究，识别出六种"偏见气味"（bias smells）作为公平性债务的早期指标，包括上下文过度简化、数据集不平衡、指标不充分、临时测试、个体多样性忽视和团队同质性，为在软件开发中将公平性纳入质量保证提供了可观测的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平性债务源于AI开发缺陷，可能导致社会危害。尽管AI公平性受到越来越多的关注，但关于从业者如何在开发过程中识别公平性债务潜在来源的证据仍然有限。缺乏对公平性债务识别指标的实证研究。

Method: 在一家组织内对四个人工智能项目进行探索性案例研究，通过25名专业人士的半结构化访谈和开放式问卷收集数据，辅以内部沟通渠道和项目文档的观察，采用迭代定性编码、备忘录和持续比较法进行分析。

Result: 识别出六种反复出现的公平性债务信号指标（偏见气味）：上下文过度简化、数据集不平衡、指标不充分、临时测试、个体多样性忽视和同质团队组成。这些气味涵盖技术层面和人类层面的软件开发方面，提示可能引入或加强偏见并导致公平性债务的条件。

Conclusion: 偏见气味将软件气味范式扩展到公平性领域，为通过可观察的指标将公平性纳入软件质量保证提供了基础。

Abstract: Context: Fairness debt arises from AI development shortcomings that may lead to societal harms. While technical and social debt concern software design decisions and team dynamics, respectively, fairness debt captures the long-term consequences of development decisions that may reinforce bias and inequities in AI-based software systems. Despite growing attention to AI fairness, limited evidence exists on how practitioners recognize potential sources of fairness debt during development. Aim: This study investigates the indicators practitioners recognize as signaling potential sources of fairness debt in AI-based software projects. Method: We conducted an exploratory case study of four AI projects within one organization. Data were collected from 25 professionals through semi-structured interviews and open-ended questionnaires, complemented by observation of internal communication channels and project documentation, and analyzed using iterative qualitative coding, memoing, and constant comparison. Results: We identified six recurring indicators, termed bias smells: Context Oversimplification, Dataset Imbalance, Metrics Inadequacies, Ad hoc Testing, Individual Diversity Unawareness, and Homogeneous Team Composition. These smells span technical and human aspects of software development and signal conditions that may introduce or reinforce bias and contribute to fairness debt. Conclusion: Bias smells extend the software smell paradigm to fairness and provide a foundation for incorporating fairness into software quality assurance through observable indicators.

</details>


### [186] [Enhancing Reliability of Symbolic Execution Tools for Smart Contract Analysis through Rule-Based False Positive Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10265)
*Muhammad Ali Hassan Ahmad,Muhammad Hashim Ali,Muhammad Ali Amer,Muhammad Naiman Jalil,Muhammad Hassan,Affan Rauf*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对以太坊智能合约安全分析工具Mythril（基于符号执行）产生大量误报的问题，通过分析误报的根本原因，设计并实现了一种基于规则的方法来减少误报。实验结果表明，该方法在保持真实漏洞检出率的同时显著降低了误报率，提升了工具的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约安全性至关重要，但现有的基于符号执行的漏洞检测工具常常产生大量误报，导致安全分析人员将时间和精力浪费在调查虚假警报上，从而分散了对真实漏洞的关注。因此，必须改进工具算法和启发式规则，以区分真实漏洞与误报。

Method: 首先展示Mythril（以太坊智能合约的符号执行分析工具）漏洞报告中误报的普遍性；分析这些误报产生的根本原因；基于分析所得洞察设计一套规则化方法；针对Mythril中影响最大的漏洞类型实现这些规则，并评估方法的有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该规则化方法在不影响真实漏洞检测的前提下，显著减少了误报数量，从而增强了Mythril工具的可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过基于误报根因分析的规则化方法，可以有效减少符号执行工具产生的误报，在保证真实漏洞检出能力的同时提高智能合约安全分析工具的实用性，为后续改进此类工具提供了有价值的思路。

Abstract: A blockchain is a decentralized, secure ledger system that enables transparent and immutable record-keeping, essential for trust and security in digital transactions. Smart contracts are self-executing agreements encoded on a blockchain, enabling different parties to fulfill the terms of the agreement automatically. These contracts trigger corresponding actions when conditions are met, ensuring decentralized and transparent transactions. Writing reliable smart contracts is challenging due to the lack of standardization. To find security vulnerabilities, tools based on various approaches, including symbolic execution, are used. However, these tools often report a large number of false positives, raising concerns about their reliability. The time and effort spent investigating false positives diverts resources from addressing actual vulnerabilities. Therefore, such tools must also be evaluated according to the rate of false positives they exhibit. More importantly, the algorithms and heuristics used by the tools must be enhanced to distinguish between true vulnerabilities and false alarms. In this paper, we first demonstrate the prevalence of false positives in vulnerability reports generated by Mythril, a symbolic execution-based analysis tool for Ethereum smart contracts. We analyze the root causes of these inaccuracies and devise a rule-based approach based on the gained insight to reduce false positives. We implement our rules for the most impactful vulnerabilities in Mythril and assess the effectiveness of our approach. Our results show a significant reduction in false positives without compromising the detection of true vulnerabilities, thus enhancing the tool's reliability.

</details>


### [187] [Comprendia: AI-Augmented Code Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10290)
*Costain Nachuma,Minhaz F. Zibran*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Comprendia是一个Eclipse插件，将结构化依赖图可视化与LLM代码解释集成在共享交互式图形上，用于Java程序理解。它通过多边类型依赖图、基于图感知调用者剪枝的LLM解释、克隆检测覆盖层和CVE风险覆盖层四个核心支柱，帮助开发者在保持控制的同时理解代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代码理解工具要么提供结构化的依赖关系可视化，要么提供LLM驱动的代码解释，但两者缺乏集成。此外，LLM生成的解释往往不可审计、不可复现，且与开发者正在探索的代码结构脱节，导致开发者难以信任和验证AI生成的解释。

Method: Comprendia由四根支柱组成：(1)多边类型依赖图，支持实时搜索和多种布局；(2)基于图感知调用者剪枝(GACP)的LLM解释，利用开发者正在浏览的同一张图来选取相关被调用者，图距离、继承折叠和边类型权重共同生成可复现且可追溯到图节点的提示；(3)克隆检测覆盖层，突出重复代码并建议提取到父类的重构机会；(4)基于OSV.dev的CVE风险覆盖层。

Result: 作者在包含已知克隆和漏洞的Java项目上演示了Comprendia，展示了统一图基座如何支持代码理解，同时让开发者保持控制。论文提供了截屏视频链接供进一步验证。

Conclusion: Comprendia通过将结构化依赖图与LLM解释无缝集成，实现了可审计、可复现的AI辅助代码理解，并通过克隆检测和安全漏洞覆盖层进一步增强了其实用性，证明了统一图基座是支持开发者理解Java项目的有效途径。

Abstract: Comprendia is an Eclipse plugin that integrates structural dependency visualization with LLM-powered code explanation on a shared interactive graph for Java program comprehension. The tool rests on four pillars: (1) a multi-edge-type dependency graph with live search and multiple layouts; (2) LLM explanations grounded in Graph-Aware Callee Pruning (GACP), an auditable strategy that selects relevant callees using the same graph the developer navigates; (3) a clone-detection overlay that highlights duplication and suggests extract-to-parent refactoring opportunities; and (4) a CVE risk overlay powered by OSV.dev. GACP uses graph distance, inheritance collapse, and edge-type weighting to produce prompts that are reproducible across LLM families and traceable to visible graph nodes. We demonstrate Comprendia on a Java project containing known clones and vulnerabilities, showing how the unified graph substrate supports comprehension while keeping the developer in control. Screencast: https://youtu.be/1wlh_RYehzA

</details>


### [188] [Does the way we write a theory change the program an LLM builds from it? A prospective randomized study of renderer format in LLM theory-to-program translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10314)
*Andre Panossian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过预注册随机实验考察了LLM将口头理论翻译为可执行模型时，表达格式（结构化干预合约 vs. 连续散文）是否系统性改变生成程序的行为。结果显示两个主要假设均未获支持，同账户可辨识性接近随机水平，说明格式效应并非如预期般统一且类别可分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口头理论无法直接运行，翻译为可执行模型时需要进行变量、干预和交互的选择。研究者希望检验：在理论内容完全相同的前提下，仅改变呈现格式是否会导致大语言模型生成产生系统性差异的程序，从而为LLM理论到程序的翻译设定边界。

Method: 采用前瞻性预注册随机实验设计：16个独立分配比特将32个配对渲染器槽位分配到结构化干预合约与连续散文两种格式；两个固定的LLM快照翻译5个匿名理论账户，共生成320个预授权的单次程序；确定性评估器测量原子有限差分响应（H1）和混合交互响应（H2）；两个主要终点评估跨模型匹配距离缩减和闭集同账户可辨识性，使用精确随机化推断和27条预注册支持标准，覆盖符号线性与量级排名两条分析流程。

Result: H1与H2均返回注册裁定NOT_SUPPORTED，108项标准评估中仅19项通过。同账户可辨识性接近随机水平（AUC 0.469-0.523，注册阈值为0.80）。一个H2匹配距离终点在符号线性流程中显著并通过多重性校正，但对应的量级排名结果未达注册效应量下限，未满足联合支持规则。

Conclusion: 渲染格式并未产生预先预测的统一的、家族不变的、可分类的行为几何。该结果对LLM理论到程序翻译中的规范格式效应设置了具体边界，并提供了完全可审计的随机化设计、数据集和软件供后续研究使用。

Abstract: A verbal theory does not run: translating it into an executable model requires choices about variables, interventions, and interactions. We tested whether the presentation of otherwise identical theoretical content systematically changes the programs produced by large language models. In a prospective preregistered randomized experiment, 16 independent assignment bits allocated 32 paired renderer slots between a structured intervention contract and connected prose. Two pinned LLM snapshots translated five anonymous theoretical accounts, yielding 320 preauthorized single-shot programs in a frozen sparse quadratic language. A deterministic evaluator measured atomic finite-difference responses (H1) and mixed interaction responses (H2). Two primary endpoints assessed cross-model matched-distance reduction and closed-set same-account identifiability, with exact randomization inference and 27 preregistered support criteria evaluated across signed-linear and magnitude-rank pipelines. Both H1 and H2 returned the registered verdict NOT_SUPPORTED; only 19 of 108 criterion evaluations passed. Same-account identifiability remained near chance (AUC 0.469-0.523, against a registered 0.80 threshold). One H2 matched-distance endpoint moved and survived multiplicity correction in the signed-linear pipeline, but the corresponding magnitude-rank result missed the registered effect-size floor, so it did not satisfy the joint support rule. Thus renderer format did not produce the uniform, family-invariant, classifiable behavioral geometry predicted in advance. The result places a concrete boundary on specification-format effects in LLM theory-to-program translation and provides a fully auditable randomized design, datasets, and software for studying executable formalization.

</details>


### [189] [Do Personalized Skills Help Coding Agents? An Empirical Study of Developer Interaction Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10319)
*Shuyan Huang,Kai Du,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，从开发者与LLM代理的交互历史中提取可复用的开发者偏好技能，并通过可复现的回放框架和LLM模拟开发者进行评估。在206个真实开发者会话上的实验表明，个性化技能相比无技能基线提升有限且不稳定，而跨开发者汇总的通用技能提升最大且最稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理在软件工程中广泛应用，开发者通过与代理的反复交互产生个性化偏好。现有工作主要关注任务特定技能，尚不清楚从交互历史中蒸馏的开发者特定技能能否泛化到未来任务，因此需要系统性研究开发者个性化技能的有效性。

Method: 提出框架包括：(1) 通过基于规则的引导和基于证据的细化生成个性化技能；(2) 使用可复现的回放框架，结合交互式、轨迹条件的LLM人类开发者模拟器进行评估。实验在206个真实开发者-代理会话（13位开发者）上，比较个性化技能与无技能、通用技能和其他用户技能基线。

Result: 个性化技能相比无技能基线仅带来微小且不一致的改进，而跨开发者汇总的通用技能获得最大且最一致的收益。进一步分析表明，当开发者的偏好频繁出现且历史中包含与未来任务相关的多个示例时，个性化技能更有效。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了开发者特定个性化何时有效的条件，并证明可广泛迁移的程序性知识比开发者特定偏好信号更稳健，为开发者-代理协作中的技能设计提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered agents have rapidly evolved from code-completion tools into solvers of complex software engineering tasks. As developers collaborate with coding agents over time, their preferences emerge through repeated interactions and can be used to adapt agent behavior to better meet individual developers' needs. Capturing and reusing these preferences may reduce repeated corrections and improve developer-agent collaboration. Agent skills provide a lightweight mechanism for transferring experience without modifying model parameters. However, existing work primarily focuses on task-specific skills, and it remains unclear whether developer-specific skills distilled from interaction histories can generalize to future tasks. We propose a framework for extracting reusable developer preferences from interaction traces. It first generates personalized skills through rule-based bootstrapping and evidence-grounded refinement, and then evaluates them using a reproducible replay framework with an interactive, trajectory-conditioned LLM-based human developer simulator. We conduct an experiment on 206 real-world developer-agent sessions from 13 developers and compare personalized skills against no-skill, generic-skill, and other-user-skill baselines. Personalized skills provide small and inconsistent improvements over the no-skill baseline, whereas generic skills pooled across developers achieve the largest and most consistent gains. Further analysis suggests that personalized skills become more effective when developer preferences appear frequently, particularly when their histories contain multiple examples relevant to future tasks. These findings provide empirical insights into when developer-specific personalization is effective and demonstrate that broadly transferable procedural knowledge can be more robust than developer-specific preference signals.

</details>


### [190] [Persistent Recursive Worlds Enable Autonomous Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10450)
*Beichen Huang,Zhenyu Liang,Bowen Zheng,Ran Cheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍EvoX Genesis——一种将软件视为持久递归世界而非依赖持久智能体的软件系统组织方式。有限生命智能体只提出局部改动，递归委托跨路径协作，仅接受的结果推进持久版本历史。该系统用DeepSeek V4 Flash构建了约25万行代码的Rust C编译器（耗资仅44美元，历时120小时），通过完整c-testsuite及大部分LLVM/Csmith测试，并在多个基准上实现了1.55-6.87倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂软件系统的开发周期远超任何单个编码智能体的寿命。现有的智能体系统依赖持久会话、记忆、管理器或共享上下文来维持连续性，这本质上依赖"持久智能体"这一前提。本文提出相反的思路：让软件项目本身持久，而局部智能体保持有限寿命，从而解决长期软件开发的连续性问题。

Method: Genesis将软件表示为持久递归世界：每个局部世界由被接受的版本和仓库路径定位；有限生命智能体提出局部改动；递归委托允许工作在不同路径之间移动；只有被接受的后果才能推进持久的版本历史。这种组织方式将"项目持久性"与"智能体短暂性"解耦。

Result: 在编译器形成方面，Genesis用DeepSeek V4 Flash构建了约25万行Rust C编译器，耗时120小时，存档超1000次智能体会话，仅花费44美元，且通过了完整c-testsuite和大部分LLVM/Csmith测试。在编译器延续方面，用GLM 5.2生成的编译器世界在反复替换智能体后仍保持全部测试性能。在再开发方面，将13个MESA模块（超10万行Fortran）重写为近9万行Rust，六个数值工作负载中位数加速1.55-6.87倍。

Conclusion: 长途技术软件开发可以围绕持久项目而非持久智能体来组织。这一方法在编译器构建、智能体替换延续和大型代码库再开发三个场景中都验证了可行性，展示了低成本、高可持续性的智能体软件工程新范式。

Abstract: Complex software systems develop over timescales that exceed the lifespan of any individual coding agent. Most agentic software systems preserve continuity through persistent sessions, memories, managers or shared context. We introduce EvoX Genesis (hereafter, Genesis), which instead makes the software project persistent while allowing local agents to remain finite-lived. Genesis represents software as a persistent recursive world: each local world is situated by an accepted version and a repository path, finite-lived agents propose local changes, recursive delegation moves work across paths, and only accepted consequences advance the persistent version history. We evaluate this organization across formation, continuation and redevelopment. Starting from a repository with no compiler implementation, Genesis used DeepSeek V4 Flash to build a Rust-based C compiler with about 250k tracked lines; the run lasted over 120 hours, archived over 1,000 agent episodes and incurred only US$44 in model-token charges. The compiler passed the complete c-testsuite and most LLVM and Csmith tests. In a separate compiler world generated with GLM 5.2, development continued after repeated agent replacement while retaining full test performance. Genesis also reimplemented 13 MESA modules with over 100k Fortran lines as a Rust workspace with nearly 90k Rust lines; across six numerical workloads, it achieved median speedups of 1.55--6.87x. These results show that long-horizon software development can be organized around a persistent project rather than a persistent agent.

</details>


### [191] [CausalRepair: Bridging the Causality Gap in Large Language Model-Based Automated Program Repair via Dual-Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10613)
*Linhao Wu,Yizhou Chen,Zhen Yang,Pengyu Xue,Dan Hao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CausalRepair是一种基于最小因果上下文的对话驱动自动化程序修复框架，采用双切片策略（上下文感知静态切片和基于执行轨迹的动态切片）来构建精确的因果相关上下文，指导LLM进行迭代修复，在Defects4J数据集上修复了313个bug，平均修复成本仅$0.029/bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的程序修复方法存在因果差距：测试上下文可能嘈杂或不完整，而静态分析得到的源代码上下文常包含无关和未执行的代码，导致LLM难以识别真正的故障根因。

Method: 提出CausalRepair框架，采用双切片策略：上下文感知静态切片用于净化测试语义，执行轨迹动态切片用于捕获源代码中的精确运行时依赖，二者共同构建紧凑且因果相关的上下文来引导迭代修复。

Result: 在Defects4J V1.2、V2.0和Defects4J-Trans上使用DeepSeek-V3评估，正确修复了313个bug，优于ReinFix和TSAPR等最先进方法，同时将平均修复成本降至$0.029/bug。

Conclusion: 通过最小化因果上下文，CausalRepair有效弥合了LLM修复方法与真实故障根因之间的因果差距，在修复效果和成本效率上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) has recently benefited from Large Language Models (LLMs), yet their effectiveness heavily depends on repair context. Existing LLM-based APR methods suffer from a causality gap: test contexts can be noisy or incomplete, while source contexts derived from static analysis often contain irrelevant and unexecuted code, misleading LLMs from identifying the true root cause. To address this issue, we propose CausalRepair, a conversation-driven APR framework based on minimal causal context, i.e., the essential dependencies required to explain a failure. CausalRepair employs a dual-slicing strategy: context-aware static slicing purifies test semantics, while execution-trace-based dynamic slicing captures precise runtime dependencies in source code. Together, they construct compact, causally relevant contexts to guide iterative repair. We evaluate CausalRepair on Defects4J V1.2, V2.0, and Defects4J-Trans using DeepSeek-V3. CausalRepair correctly fixes 313 bugs on Defects4J, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches such as ReinFix and TSAPR, while reducing the average repair cost to $0.029 per bug.

</details>


### [192] [A Study of Cursorrules Files in GitHub Open Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10622)
*Shuang Sun,Jafar Akhoundali,Arina Kudriavtseva,Sengim Karayalcin,Olga Gadyatskaya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Cursor AI编程编辑器中的配置文件提示词（.cursorrules文件）进行了实证研究，收集并分析了来自11,427个GitHub仓库的12,110个配置文件，揭示了其分布、演化与维护特征，并开发了包含65个代码的编码本，以理解开发者如何表达编程意图、项目上下文、工程实践和安全考虑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI辅助编程日益普及且提示词工程受到广泛关注，但以往研究主要集中于对话式提示词，对配置风格的提示词文件（如.cursorrules）缺乏系统性研究，存在明显的研究空白。

Method: 收集并分析12,110个.cursorrules文件（来自11,427个GitHub仓库），进行定量分布/演化/维护分析；同时对65个提示词文件进行定性分析，构建包含65个代码的编码本。

Result: 发现.cursorrules文件自2024年中期迅速兴起，但主要集中于小型、低活跃度、单一维护者的仓库，倾向玩具项目而非专业开发；内容以代码质量和工程实践、项目结构与配置、可维护性为主，安全相关内容出现频率较低；并发现废弃的.cursorrules与现行.mdc文件之间存在主题连续性。

Conclusion: 配置文件提示词在AI编程中扮演重要角色但其应用仍以非专业场景为主，且安全相关指导严重不足；旧版.cursorrules与新版.mdc文件之间的主题延续性说明该领域正在演化但核心关注点保持一致。

Abstract: Prompts are the primary mechanism for communicating with AI agents, and they directly influence the quality and reliability of AI-generated code. As AI-assisted programming becomes widely adopted, modern tools increasingly combine dynamic conversational prompts with static configuration-like prompt files. Despite the growing focus on prompt engineering, prior research has primarily focused on conversational prompts, while prompt files remain understudied.
  To address this gap, we conduct an empirical study of configuration prompt files in Cursor, a widely used AI-assisted code editor. We collect and analyze over 12,110 .cursorrules files from 11,427 GitHub repositories to characterize their distribution, evolution, and maintenance. Complementing this, we perform qualitative analysis on a random sample of 65 prompt files and develop a 65-code codebook capturing how developers express programming intent, project context, engineering practices, and security considerations.
  Our results show that .cursorrules files emerged rapidly from mid-2024. Their adoption is concentrated in small-scale, low-activity, single-maintainer repositories, suggesting toy projects rather than professional development. The content of prompt files is dominated by guidance on code quality and engineering practices, project structure and configuration, and maintainability, while security-related content appears less frequently. Our analysis shows that there is a continuity of themes and topics between the now-legacy .cursorrules files and the current standard .mdc files.

</details>


### [193] [LLM Ensemble Fault Classification for Automotive HiL Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10710)
*Hamza Ouarrad,Mohammad Abboush,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可解释的多大语言模型集成框架，用于汽车HiL验证中的传感器级故障分类。该框架利用故障注入记录的紧凑证据表示，组合异构LLM的输出。在汽油发动机和电动汽车HiL系统上评估，Top-3集成（Mistral Small 24B、Qwen2.5 32B、Phi-4 14B）通过置信度加权投票达到0.917 Top-1准确率和0.902 MCC，优于单一模型，且Top-5集成不优于Top-3，表明模型互补性比集成规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车HiL验证产生大规模多变量测试记录，传统分析依赖人工审查和基于规则的方法，效率低且局限明显。现有机器学习和深度学习方法虽改善了故障诊断，但需要大量标注数据、跨工况泛化能力差，且预测缺乏可解释性。因此需要一种既能提供鲁棒诊断又具备可解释性的方案。

Method: 提出可解释的多LLM集成框架，包含两个关键环节：(1) 将故障注入记录转换为紧凑的证据表示，作为LLM输入；(2) 组合多个异构大语言模型（不同规模、不同架构）的输出，采用置信度加权投票策略进行最终决策。在三种驾驶场景和十个单故障类别上对汽油发动机和电动汽车HiL系统进行评估。

Result: 单个模型中Mistral Small 24B表现最优（Top-1准确率0.903，MCC 0.887，Brier分数最低0.102）。Top-3集成（Mistral Small 24B、Qwen2.5 32B、Phi-4 14B）通过置信度加权投票将场景平均结果提升至Top-1准确率0.917、宏F1 0.913、MCC 0.902，且校准性能最佳。Top-5集成未超过Top-3配置，说明模型互补性比集成规模更关键。

Conclusion: 协调的多LLM推理能够为汽车HiL验证提供鲁棒、校准良好且工程师可解释的故障分类。该研究证明模型之间的互补性比模型数量更重要，为工业场景中可解释的智能诊断提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Automotive HiL validation generates large multivariate test recordings whose analysis remains challenging due to manual review effort, rule-based limitations, and the need for explainable diagnostic decisions. Recent machine-learning and deep-learning approaches have improved fault diagnosis, but they often require large labelled datasets, generalise poorly across operating conditions, and provide limited insight into their predictions. This paper proposes an explainable multi-LLM ensemble framework for sensor-level fault classification in automotive validation. The framework uses compact evidence representations of fault-injection recordings and combines the outputs of heterogeneous large language models to improve diagnostic robustness, ranking quality, confidence reliability, and interpretability. The approach is evaluated on gasoline-engine and electric-vehicle HiL systems across three driving settings and ten single-fault classes. Among the individual models, Mistral Small~24B provides the strongest overall single-model trade-off, achieving 0.903 Top-1 accuracy, 0.887 MCC, and the lowest Brier score of 0.102. The final Top-3 ensemble combines Mistral Small~24B, Qwen2.5~32B, and Phi-4~14B using confidence-weighted voting, improving the scenario-averaged results to 0.917 Top-1 accuracy, 0.913 macro F1, and 0.902 MCC, while also providing the best calibration among the tested ensemble strategies. A Top-5 ensemble does not improve over the Top-3 configuration, indicating that model complementarity is more important than ensemble size. The results show that coordinated multi-LLM reasoning can support robust, calibrated, and engineer-interpretable fault classification for automotive HiL validation.

</details>


### [194] [Conflict Extraction in Probabilistic Datalog Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10755)
*Siyu Chen,Chungha Sung,Xuyang Li,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了PPProbe，一个专门针对概率Datalog分析的工具，用于提取最小不可满足子集（MUSes）以识别相互不一致的告警组合。PPProbe利用Datalog推导图的结构特性，引导搜索朝向可能的冲突并通过自底向上的UNSAT推理剪枝搜索空间。在70个基准测试上，PPProbe比现有最先进的MUS枚举器快2.5至24倍，并能过滤掉平均47.7%的相互不一致告警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率Datalog扩展使静态分析能够按可能性对告警排序，但引入了确定性分析中不存在的新挑战：最终输出可能包含单独看似合理但相互不一致的告警，因为边缘概率不保证联合可满足性。这导致开发者可能花费精力调查在任何可能世界中都不会共存的告警组合。

Method: PPProbe将不一致性形式化为最小不可满足子集（MUSes），并利用Datalog推导图的结构来引导搜索朝向可能的冲突，同时通过自底向上的UNSAT推理来剪枝搜索空间。该方法专注于结构感知的冲突提取，而非通用目的地的MUS枚举改进。

Result: 在70个基准测试（覆盖功率侧信道分析、数据竞争检测、语义差异比较和贝叶斯网络推理）上，PPProbe相比最先进的MUS枚举器实现了2.5到24倍的吞吐量提升，识别出的冲突提供保守的误报减少估计，平均过滤47.7%的相互不一致告警。

Conclusion: PPProbe通过利用Datalog推导图的结构特性，有效解决了概率Datalog分析中告警之间相互不一致的问题，大幅提升了冲突提取的效率，并显著减少了开发者需要调查的无效告警组合。

Abstract: Probabilistic extensions of Datalog enable static analyses such as pointer analysis, data race detection, and side-channel analysis to rank alarms by likelihood, but this added expressiveness also introduces a new challenge absent from deterministic analyses: the final output may contain alarms that are individually plausible yet mutually inconsistent, because marginal probabilities do not guarantee joint satisfiability. As a result, developers may spend effort investigating combinations of alarms that can never co-occur in any possible world. We address this problem by formalizing such inconsistencies as minimal unsatisfiable subsets (MUSes) and introducing PPProbe, a conflict extractor specialized for probabilistic Datalog analyses. Rather than improving MUS enumeration in general, PPProbe exploits the structure of Datalog derivation graphs to guide the search toward likely conflicts and prune the search space through bottom-up UNSAT inference. We evaluate PPProbe on 70 benchmarks from power side-channel analysis, data race detection, semantic diffing, and Bayesian-network inference. The results show that PPProbe achieves 2.5 to 24 times higher throughput than state-of-the-art MUS enumerators, and that the conflicts it identifies yield a conservative estimate of false-positive reduction, filtering out an average of 47.7% of mutually inconsistent alarms.

</details>


### [195] [Extending Triple Graph Grammars to Formalize Complex View Definitions on Families of Models - Long Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10785)
*Lars König,Jens Kosiol*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文延续了为NeoJoin视图定义语言赋予基于三元图语法（TGGs）的正式语义的研究工作，并针对重叠查询翻译的便利性问题，引入了TGG规则的跳跃语义（skip semantics），从而支持模型与视图之间的自动化和增量同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视图的开发是管理复杂系统和促进协作的重要技术，但视图定义语言需要兼具易用性和精确的形式语义。当前工作中TGGs在支持重叠查询翻译方面存在缺口，需要加以解决。

Method: 延续先前基于TGGs为NeoJoin视图定义语言赋予形式语义的工作，识别出重叠查询翻译支持不足的缺口，并提出为TGG规则引入跳跃语义（skip semantics），允许规则根据应用上下文跳过某些规定的动作。

Result: 通过引入skip semantics弥补了TGGs在重叠查询翻译支持方面的不足，同时为NeoJoin语言提供了具备高形式保证的自动化和增量同步机制，也进一步验证了TGGs的表达力和可用性。

Conclusion: 本文工作为基于视图的开发提供了更完善的正式语义支撑，使模型与视图之间的同步过程自动化且具有高形式保证，同时为TGGs的应用提供了一例新的案例研究。

Abstract: View-based development is an important technique to manage complexity and facilitate cooperation in the development of modern and complex systems of systems. This approach places high demands on view definition languages used in the development process. First, such a language needs to be approachable and extremely versatile so that developers from diverse backgrounds are able to use it and can define views for a diverse set of different tasks. Yet, the language also needs to have a precise and formal semantics so that it is possible to integrate the work performed on various views in a controlled manner to obtain a coherent overall system. In this paper, we continue previous work of equipping the NeoJoin view definition language with a formal semantics that is based on triple graph grammars (TGGs). This paves the way for obtaining automated and incremental synchronization procedures between models and views that come with high formal guarantees for their behavior. Simultaneously, our work serves as a further case study of the expressivity and usability of TGGs.
