We identify one gap, namely convenient support for the translation of overlapping queries from the view definition language, and tackle that gap by introducing a skip semantics for TGG rules, i.e., allowing to skip certain actions a rule prescribes, depending on the application context.

</details>


### [196] [From Pattern Detection to Composition Analysis in Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10882)
*Neilson Carlos Leite Ramalho,Erico Augusto da Silva,Anthony Accioly,Higor Amario de Souza,Marcos Lordello Chaim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文扩展了量子软件模式挖掘工具，增加了两个匹配通道和词汇扩展步骤，在Qrisp框架上达到0.712的micro-F1分数，并构建了组合图来研究模式实现如何在不同框架内组装及共现原因，最终发布了开源挖掘管道qpa和相关数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件模式在实践中如何被采纳仍缺乏实证证据，尽管量子模式目录已存在，但自动化挖掘和模式间关系的研究不足。

Method: 在原有61模式目录基础上，扩展工具增加两个匹配通道和词汇扩展步骤；构建组合图记录与模式相关的高层框架组件间的调用关系并存储在图数据库中；使用LLM集成对新增组件进行分类维护知识库。

Result: 在Qrisp框架上达到0.712的micro-F1分数，而未经词汇扩展时为0.449；知识库映射了5个来源的286个框架组件到模式目录。

Conclusion: 通过组合图分析模式实现如何在框架内组装、为何共现，并区分开发者直接调用与内部框架调用引入的模式，最终提供可复现的qpa管道和数据集以支持量子模式采纳与演化的研究。

Abstract: Quantum software patterns provide high-level abstractions for building quantum programs, but there is still little empirical evidence on how they are adopted in practice. In prior work, we extended an existing quantum-pattern atlas into a 61-pattern catalog, created a knowledge base that links framework components to those patterns, and built a tool that mines pattern implementations from open-source code. We applied this tool on 80 projects and find that all 23 patterns occur in practice. In this work, we extend the tool with two additional matching channels and a vocabulary expansion step, and execute a quantitative evaluation of its accuracy on Qrisp, a framework not present in the knowledge base, reaching a micro-F1 of 0.712 against 0.449 without the expansion step. We then construct composition graphs that record calls among the high-level framework components associated with patterns and store them in a graph database. We use these graphs to examine how pattern implementations are assembled inside each framework, why patterns co-occur, and how much of a pattern's detection count comes from components called directly by developers rather than introduced through internal framework calls. We release qpa, an open-source mining pipeline, together with the knowledge base, which maps 286 framework components across five sources to the pattern catalog, maintained with the support of an LLM ensemble that classifies newly extracted components, and the resulting pattern usage dataset, to support reproducible studies on the adoption and evolution of quantum patterns.

</details>


### [197] [GitSkills: A Dataset of Agent Skills on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10906)
*Giuseppe Destefanis,Daniel Graziotin,Matteo Vaccargiu,Marco Ortu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍GitSkills数据集，包含从282,200个公共GitHub仓库中收集的3,797,117个SKILL.md文件，用于研究AI代理技能（agent skills）的编写、复用与维护方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent技能（SKILL.md文件夹格式）在2025年10月由Anthropic提出后迅速普及，九个月内已有数百万技能文件出现在公共GitHub仓库中。然而这类技能文件与传统的软件工程构件不同：主要用自然语言编写、由模型在运行时概率性选择、无编译器或类型检查器验证、无中央注册表或包管理器，因此需要实证研究其开发、复用与维护模式，但现有数据集中没有记录这一群体。

Method: 作者构建了GitSkills数据集：从282,200个公共仓库中收集3,797,117个SKILL.md文件，保留每次文件出现（含仓库、路径、内容哈希），将相同文件分组为1,877,981个不同内容，并为每组代表性文件增加全文、解析的前置元数据、文件夹内容、仓库元数据及部分文件的提交历史，整合为单一自包含的SQLite文件。

Result: GitSkills数据集包含超过379万个SKILL.md文件，来自28万多个公共仓库，去重后约188万个不同内容，并附带丰富的元数据和提交历史信息。

Conclusion: GitSkills为研究代理技能的采纳、复用、结构、作者归属、维护与安全等方面提供了全面的数据基础，填补了该领域实证研究的空白。

Abstract: An agent skill is a folder containing a SKILL.md file with instructions for a language-model agent, optionally accompanied by scripts and reference files. The agent loads the skill when it judges that a task matches the skill description. Anthropic introduced the format in October 2025 as an open specification. Nine months later, we find that skill files in the millions sit in public GitHub repositories. Skills are unlike the artifacts the SE research community usually mines: they are written mainly in natural language, a model selects them probabilistically at run time, and no compiler or type checker verifies the selection. They also have no central registry or package manager, so they spread by copying folders between repositories. How developers write, reuse, and maintain skills is therefore an empirical question, and no existing dataset records this population. We present GitSkills, a dataset of 3,797,117 SKILL.md files collected from 282,200 public repositories in July 2026. The dataset retains every file occurrence with its repository, path, and content hash. It groups identical files into 1,877,981 distinct contents and enriches one representative per group with the full text, parsed front matter, folder contents, repository metadata, and, for a subset, the commit history of the file. A single self- contained SQLite file supports research on the adoption, reuse, structure, authorship, maintenance, and security of agent skills.

</details>


### [198] [Understanding the Architecture of Coding Agents: An Exploratory Study Using a Research Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10934)
*Marco Tulio Valente*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统性地记录了编码智能体（coding agents）的主要架构组件，并提出了Ark（Agent Research Kit）——一个极简开源编码智能体，以及ArkBench轻量级基准测试集。在10个软件维护与演化任务中，Ark使用gpt-5.4-mini成功解决了8个任务，并通过架构分类法与最先进的编码智能体进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体已成为AI辅助软件开发的主要接口，但其内部架构鲜为人知，缺乏像编译器或操作系统那样系统化的架构描述。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者记录了编码智能体的核心架构组件及其交互与执行流程；开发了Ark开源编码智能体以保留现代编码智能体的关键架构机制，同时强调简单性与清晰度；引入了ArkBench基准（包含10个软件维护和演化任务）；并使用最近提出的架构分类法与SOTA编码智能体进行对比。

Result: 使用gpt-5.4-mini模型，Ark在ArkBench的10个任务中成功解决了8个，且token消耗适中。架构对比也已完成。

Conclusion: Ark与ArkBench为编码智能体的教学、研究和实验提供了实用基础，有助于弥补该领域系统性架构知识缺失的问题。

Abstract: Coding agents have rapidly emerged as the primary interface for AI-assisted software development. However, despite their growing adoption, relatively little is known about their internal architecture, and no systematic architectural description comparable to those available for compilers or operating systems currently exists. This paper addresses this gap by documenting the main architectural components of coding agents, explaining their responsibilities, interactions, and execution flow. To support this effort, we also present Ark (Agent Research Kit), a minimal open-source coding agent designed for research and education that preserves the essential architectural mechanisms of modern coding agents while emphasizing simplicity and clarity. We also introduce ArkBench, a lightweight benchmark comprising ten representative software maintenance and evolution tasks. Using gpt-5.4-mini, Ark successfully solved 8 of the 10 tasks while requiring modest token consumption. Finally, we compare the architecture of Ark with those of state-of-the-art coding agents using a recently proposed architectural taxonomy. We hope that both Ark and ArkBench provide a practical foundation for teaching, research, and experimentation on coding agents.

</details>


### [199] [Agentic Configuration Management (ACM): A Reference Configuration Model for Governed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11166)
*Audrey Quessada-Vial*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic配置管理（ACM）框架，用于对异构Agent系统进行统一配置治理，通过语义投影将不同框架的原生配置规范化为统一配置图，并提供Python参考实现及跨框架的治理一致性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMOps和AgentOps平台仅支持编排与可观测性，缺乏统一的配置治理模型来对由异构Agent、提示词、工具、模型等多元素组成的Agent系统进行一致、版本化的配置表示与管理。

Method: 提出ACM参考模型，包含类型化且独立版本化的配置项、不可变修订与基线、显式配置-运行时分离、生命周期与保障语义、依赖感知的影响传播及运行时溯源。通过语义投影将异构原生配置规范化为统一的配置图（Configuration Graph），在此图上执行通用治理语义，并以Python实现适配LangGraph、CrewAI和OpenAI Agents SDK。

Result: 基于27个治理场景和9个量化影响传播案例的评估表明，三个框架在投影后产生治理等价的ACM表示和可复现的治理结果。影响语义被形式化为有限格上的单调传播，确立了收敛性、终止性及初始影响估值之上最小不动点的唯一性。

Conclusion: 通用治理语义能够在评估范围内支持跨异构Agent执行抽象的可复现性、可审计性、依赖分析和互操作性。核心贡献包括统一的配置治理参考模型、形式化的影响传播语义及其理论基础。

Abstract: Agentic systems are increasingly composed of heterogeneous agents, prompts, tools, models, skills, composite subsystems, policies, and execution workflows whose configurations evolve across frameworks and runtime environments. Existing LLMOps and AgentOps platforms support orchestration and observability but do not provide a common configuration-governance model for representing and governing these systems as coherent, versioned configurations.
  This paper introduces Agentic Configuration Management (ACM), a framework-independent governance and configuration reference model for heterogeneous agentic systems. ACM combines typed and independently versioned Agentic Configuration Items, immutable revisions and baselines, explicit configuration-runtime separation, lifecycle and assurance semantics, dependency-aware impact propagation, and runtime provenance. Heterogeneous native configurations are normalized through semantic projection into a canonical Configuration Graph on which common governance semantics operate.
  We provide a Python reference implementation with adapters for LangGraph, CrewAI, and the OpenAI Agents SDK. The evaluation combines 27 governance scenarios with nine quantitative impact-propagation cases. For the evaluated configurations, the three frameworks yield governance-equivalent ACM representations and reproducible governance outcomes after projection. The impact semantics are formalized as monotone propagation over a finite lattice, establishing convergence, termination, and uniqueness of the least fixed point above the initial impact valuation. These results provide evidence that common governance semantics can support reproducibility, auditability, dependency analysis, and interoperability across heterogeneous agentic execution abstractions within the evaluated scope.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [World-First SEM-based Recovery of Crash EDR Data from the EEPROM of a Severely Damaged SRS Module Using CrashScan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10152)
*Sergei Skorobogatov,Peter Vertal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种经济可行的方法，用于在汽车SRS（安全气囊控制）模块存储芯片永久损坏后恢复数据。通过对严重事故中处理器和Flash EEPROM芯片均遭电损伤（芯片封装有可见孔洞）的真实模块进行实验，成功以100%的成功率提取了所有关键信息。研究表明损伤由过电压引发的大电流导致局部温度超过3000°C，尽管EEPROM阵列距损坏区域仅几百微米，却未丢失任何数据位。恢复后的数据通过Collision Sciences CrashScan进行标准EDR提取，生成了完整的EDR报告。该创新性的样品制备和成像技术为未来从严重损坏设备中恢复数据提供了可靠的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前，如果事故导致存储器芯片损坏，没有可行或经济实惠的方案来完整提取或恢复数据。作者旨在填补这一空白，为数据恢复提供一种新的方法论。

Method: 采用创新的专有样品制备和成像技术，从芯片封装受损（包含物理孔洞）、内部金属线熔化和硅基底部分蒸发的严重损坏SRS模块中提取数据。利用损坏区域的物理特征分析（表明温度超过3000°C），并借助供体单元将恢复的数据通过Collision Sciences CrashScan进行标准EDR提取。

Result: 成功以100%的成功率从严重损坏（处理器和Flash EEPROM芯片均遭电气损伤且有可见孔洞）的真实SRS模块中提取所有关键信息。EEPROM阵列尽管距损坏区域仅几百微米，但未丢失任何单个比特。通过标准EDR提取工具生成了完整的EDR事故重建报告。

Conclusion: 该方法和数据分析方法对严重损坏设备（包括机械、电气或火灾损坏）的数据恢复具有高度韧性，不仅适用于汽车行业，还可推广至医疗、航空和航天领域。

Abstract: This paper introduces fully working and affordable approach to data recovery from automotive SRS (airbag control) module after permanent failure of its data storage memory chip. All essential information was successfully extracted with 100% success rate from a real SRS module after severe accident which resulted in both its Processor and Flash EEPROM chip electrically damaged with visible holes in their packages. Further investigation revealed that the damage was likely caused by overvoltage and subsequent high-current-flow through silicon die of chips. This not only caused some internal metal wires to melt and evaporate but also caused partial evaporation of silicon substrate and the created cavity was filled with melted gold from a bonding wire. This reveals the nature of the failure with local temperatures exceeding 3000°C. Despite to the fact that the on-chip EEPROM array was only a few hundred micrometers away from the damaged area, it did not lose any single bit of information. This proved the approach to be resilient for successful data recovery from severely damaged devices in the future.
  With the donor unit containing the recovered data, standard bench-top EDR extraction was performed using Collision Sciences CrashScan, yielding a full EDR report. The forensic significance of the recovered data, the technical novelty of the methodology, and the complete accident reconstruction are presented and discussed.
  Until now, if because of an accident the memory chip was damaged, there was no feasible or affordable solution for full data extraction or recovery. With the use of innovative and proprietary sample preparation and imaging techniques, it became possible to extract and verify all the data stored inside chips after permanent mechanical, electrical or fire damage. This could help not only automotive but also medical, aviation and aerospace industries.

</details>


### [201] [MarkNull: Model-Agnostic Watermark Removal in AI-Generated Images via On-Manifold Latent Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10166)
*Jie Cao,Qi Li,Zelin Zhang,Xiaodong Wu,Lingshuang Liu,Xiangman Li,Jianbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MarkNull，一种模型无关的水印移除攻击方法，通过流形上潜变量操控来去相关水印图像中潜表征与嵌入噪声之间的统计依赖，将平均比特准确率降至53.14%（接近随机猜测50%），且不产生可感知的图像质量损失；同时提出加速变体MarkNull-A（0.50秒/图）及对应的攻击检测防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印攻击要么仅对特定生成模型有效，要么在移除水印时导致严重的视觉退化。AI生成图像的数字水印在面临真实世界、模型无关的移除攻击时的鲁棒性尚未被充分探索，亟需一种既能广泛适用又能保持图像质量的攻击框架来评估现有水印方案的脆弱性。

Method: MarkNull基于关键观察：带水印图像在生成潜表征与嵌入初始噪声之间存在强统计依赖。为此引入噪声-潜表征对齐分数（NLAS）量化该依赖，并构建优化目标，选择性地去相关潜表征与嵌入水印，同时保持语义保真度。扩展变体MarkNull-A将攻击蒸馏为单次前向传播，实现无优化的摊销攻击。

Result: 在post-hoc、微调型和初始噪声型三类水印方案上的广泛评估表明，MarkNull将平均比特准确率降至53.14%，接近50%的随机猜测水平，且无可感知的图像退化。MarkNull-A达到0.50秒/图的处理速度。攻击成功攻破Google的SynthID-Image系统并保持高视觉质量，且可迁移至视频水印。

Conclusion: 通过攻击与防御检测机制的双向验证，本文揭示了现有水印方案在模型无关潜空间攻击下的脆弱性，强调开发对潜空间攻击具有鲁棒性的水印设计的必要性。

Abstract: Digital watermarking has emerged as a critical technique for provenance and copyright attribution in AI-generated imagery, yet its robustness against realistic, model-agnostic removal attacks remains poorly explored. Existing attacks either succeed only against specific generative models or achieve removal at the cost of severe visual degradation. In this paper, we propose MarkNull, a model-agnostic watermark removal attack via on-manifold latent manipulation. MarkNull is grounded in a key observation: watermarked images exhibit a strong statistical dependency between the generated latent representation and the embedded initial noise. To quantify this dependency, we introduce the Noise-Latent Alignment Score (NLAS) and formulate an optimization objective that selectively decorrelates the latent representation from the embedded watermark while preserving semantic fidelity. Extensive evaluations across different categories of watermarking paradigms, including post-hoc, fine-tuning-based, and initial-noise-based schemes, demonstrate that MarkNull reduces average bit accuracy to 53.14%, approaching random-guessing (50%), without perceptible image degradation. To further improve scalability, we propose MarkNull-A, an amortized, optimization-free variant that distills the attack into a single forward pass, achieving 0.50 s/image with modest computational overhead. Notably, our attacks successfully compromise Google's SynthID-Image system while preserving high visual quality and transfer effectively to video watermarking. Finally, we present an attack detection mechanism as a defensive counterpart to MarkNull and MarkNull-A, highlighting the necessity of developing watermark designs resilient to model-agnostic latent-space attacks.

</details>


### [202] [Withholding the Completing Chunk: Deterministic Pair-Completion Guardrails for Streaming LLM Output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10279)
*Christopher M. Frost*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究流式语言模型输出中的释放时机问题，提出一种确定性守卫机制——当危险签名是两个词法谓词的合取时，守卫扫描累积前缀并在第一个使两个谓词同时可观察的块处拦截释放。实验表明该方法能精确匹配缓冲区扫描器并拦截所有成对补全块，但在通用语义审核上覆盖范围有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式语言模型输出存在释放时机困境：完整响应审核发生在流式文本已经逃逸之后，而反复对部分文本进行语义分类既昂贵又不稳定。需要一种低成本、确定性的边界守卫机制来解决流式输出中的风险释放问题。

Method: 构造一个确定性守卫：每个承诺的危险签名是两个词法谓词的合取。守卫在每次释放前扫描累积前缀，并在第一个使两个谓词均可观察的块处拒绝释放。实验覆盖四个签名家族、八种块大小和32次机制试验，另做512次策略对比试验（全前缀扫描、完整缓冲、512字符窗口、块局部扫描），并用Llama Guard 3 1B作为校准基线进行对照。

Result: 流式决策与缓冲扫描器完全匹配，拦截了所有成对补全块，八个单谓词对照组全部通过。512次试验中，全前缀扫描和完整缓冲检测100%配置对，512字符窗口检测96/128，块局部扫描检测38/128。固定对在338个人类安全响应中标记0个，在394个人类标注不安全响应中检测0个。Llama Guard基线将310/338安全响应分类为安全、202/394不安全响应分类为不安全。重复前缀扫描16,384字符响应耗时13.261ms至829.640ms。

Conclusion: 配对补全机制是小型固定策略的精确释放边界后盾，但不适合替代语义审核。它提供确定性的、可预测的拦截能力，但覆盖面窄、对通用危害检测能力有限。

Abstract: Streaming language-model output creates a release-timing problem: complete-response moderation acts after streamed text has escaped, whereas repeated semantic classification of partial text can be costly and unstable. We study a narrow deterministic construction in which each committed danger signature is the conjunction of two lexical predicates. The guard scans the accumulated prefix before every release and withholds the first chunk that makes both predicates observable. Across four signature families, eight chunk sizes, and 32 mechanism trials, streaming decisions matched the buffered scanner and withheld every pair-completing chunk; eight single-predicate controls passed. In a separate 512-trial strategy comparison, full-prefix scanning and complete buffering detected all configured pairs, a 512-character window detected 96/128, and chunk-local scanning detected 38/128. Fixed pairs flagged 0/338 human-derived safe responses and detected 0/394 jury-labelled unsafe responses, confirming narrow rather than general harm coverage. A calibrated official Llama Guard 3 1B baseline classified 310/338 safe responses as safe and 202/394 unsafe responses as unsafe. Repeated-prefix scanner time on 16,384-character responses ranged from 13.261 ms to 829.640 ms across tested chunk sizes. Pair completion is therefore an exact release-boundary backstop for a small fixed policy, not a substitute for semantic moderation.

</details>


### [203] [From Prompt Injection to Web Exploitation: Revisiting Classic Vulnerabilities in LLM-Integrated Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10281)
*Spiros Tsigkopoulos,Christoforos Ntantogian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统化地提出了一类新型攻击——LLM中介的Web攻击（LLM-mediated web attacks），涵盖LLM2SQLi、LLM2XSS、LLM2SSTI等八种变体，并通过TicketOracle实验平台验证了LLM2SSRF在五个攻击场景和七个LLM上的可利用性，最后从提示、模型、应用和网络四个层面提出缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛集成到Web应用中（如聊天机器人、工具调用管道和代理工作流），用户输入不仅能影响生成的文本，还能影响后端操作（如数据库查询、HTTP请求、文件操作、模板渲染和API调用），由此产生了一个未被系统研究的新攻击面。作者希望系统化地揭示和分类这一攻击面。

Method: 作者首先提出LLM中介的Web攻击概念，将其与传统Web攻击区分开，并定义了八种LLM2X攻击变体（SQLi、XSS、SSTI、命令注入、IDOR、CSRF、XXE、SSRF）。然后实现了一个基于Flask的LLM集成Web应用TicketOracle作为实验平台，针对LLM2SSRF设计了五个攻击场景，在七个不同的LLM上进行了大量实验评估。

Result: 实验发现各LLM在易受攻击性上存在显著差异，表明攻击的成功利用既取决于不安全的应用架构，也取决于模型特有的行为。此外，分析表明LLM通常不会自行制造底层漏洞，而是充当"中介层"，在某些工具启用场景下甚至充当"困惑的代理"（confused deputy），将攻击者的影响传递到信任模型生成内容的组件中。

Conclusion: LLM中介的Web攻击是一个真实且值得重视的安全威胁类别。作者从提示层、模型层、应用层和网络层四个层面提出了缓解策略，为LLM集成Web应用的安全设计提供了系统性指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly integrated into web applications through chatbots, tool-calling pipelines, and agentic workflows. In these systems, user input may influence not only generated text, but also backend actions such as database queries, HTTP requests, file operations, template rendering, or API calls. This paper introduces LLM-mediated web attacks, a class of attacks in which attacker-controlled input is transformed by an LLM-integrated application and then reaches traditional web-application sinks. We systematize this attack surface through representative LLM2X variants, including LLM2SQLi, LLM2XSS, LLM2SSTI, LLM2CommandInjection, LLM2IDOR, LLM2CSRF, LLM2XXE, and LLM2SSRF. Our analysis shows that the LLM usually does not create the underlying vulnerability itself; rather, it acts as a mediation layer, and in some tool-enabled settings as a confused deputy, carrying attacker influence into components that trust model-generated or model-influenced content. As an experimental case study, we implement TicketOracle, a Flask-based LLM-integrated web application for evaluating LLM2SSRF across five attack scenarios and seven LLMs. Our results show substantial variation in susceptibility across models, suggesting that exploitation depends both on insecure application architecture and model-specific behavior. We conclude with mitigation strategies across the prompt, model, application, and network layers.

</details>


### [204] [Beyond Detection Accuracy: Measuring Explanation Cost, Stability, and Utility for Resource-Aware IoT Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10349)
*Abdurrahman Tolay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文联合评估了物联网入侵检测中预测有效性、解释成本、局部解释稳定性及选择性解释四方面，发现XGBoost预测性能最强、Random Forest误报率最低但解释成本高，选择性解释在平衡测试下可节省28-32%计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习入侵检测研究过度关注预测精度，将解释生成视为零成本的后处理步骤，缺乏对解释成本、稳定性和选择性调用的系统评估。

Method: 构建泄漏安全的CICIoT2023数据集（采用特征哈希、去重、标签冲突去除和确定性分区），对逻辑回归、决策树、随机森林和XGBoost在自然与平衡测试分布下进行评估，测量TreeSHAP成本、预测保持扰动下的稳定性，并使用验证校准策略分配解释工作量。

Result: XGBoost整体预测性能最强，随机森林误报率最低但TreeSHAP在5000样本下需700.759秒（XGBoost仅1.471秒）。随机森林基础解释稳定性最强，XGBoost存在特征周转和归因漂移。平衡测试下约90%假阴性覆盖率可节省28-32%计算，95%覆盖率节省15-23%，自然分布下节省更小。

Conclusion: 可解释物联网入侵检测的实用价值取决于预测质量、解释成本、局部稳定性、工作负载分布和选择性调用等多维因素，而非仅检测精度。

Abstract: Machine-learning intrusion-detection studies commonly emphasize predictive accuracy while treating explanation generation as a computationally free post-processing step. This study jointly evaluates predictive effectiveness, explanation cost, local explanation stability, and selective explanation for binary Internet of Things (IoT) intrusion detection. A leakage-safe CICIoT2023 corpus was constructed using exact 39-feature hashes, non-finite-value handling, exact-feature deduplication, conservative label-collision removal, and deterministic hash-level partitioning. Logistic Regression, Decision Tree, Random Forest, and XGBoost were evaluated on natural and balanced test distributions. TreeSHAP cost was measured, stability was assessed under prediction-preserving perturbations, and validation-calibrated policies were used to allocate explanation workload. XGBoost provided the strongest overall predictive profile, while Random Forest produced the lowest false-positive rate. At 5,000 samples, TreeSHAP required 700.759 s for Random Forest and 1.471 s for XGBoost. Random Forest showed the strongest overall base-level explanation stability; XGBoost retained high rank and directional consistency but showed greater top-feature turnover and attribution-magnitude drift. On the balanced test, about 90% false-negative explanation coverage permitted 28-32% compute savings, while about 95% coverage permitted 15-23% savings. Savings were much smaller under the attack-heavy natural prevalence. These results show that operationally useful explainable IoT intrusion detection depends on predictive quality, explanation cost, local stability, workload prevalence, and selective invocation rather than detection accuracy alone.

</details>


### [205] [Synthesizing Probabilistic Saturating Counters with Differentially Private Formal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10521)
*Zhiming Chi,Lutan Zhao,Depeng Liu,Yong Li,Pengfei Yang,Bow-Yaw Wang,Rui Hou,Cheng-Chao Huang,Andrea Turrini,Lijun Zhang,Naijun Zhan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对概率饱和计数器（PSC）在分支预测器中的安全性进行了正式分析，基于差分隐私（DP）框架量化了攻击者的区分能力，并合成了在满足目标纯DP保证的同时保持竞争性预测性能的增强型PSC参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典饱和计数器是确定性的，容易受到侧信道攻击，攻击者可以操纵计数器状态并推断受害进程的分支方向。现有基于概率饱和计数器（PSC）的缓解方案仅进行了经验性评估，缺乏正式的安全保证分析。

Method: 将PSC和对应的Prime+Probe攻击策略建模为概率Moore机，推导最优攻击策略，通过差分隐私（DP）量化攻击者的区分能力；合成了满足目标纯DP保证的增强型PSC参数，并推导了稳态误预测率以评估实用性。

Result: 合成的PSC相比确定性和现有概率饱和计数器，提供了正式的安全保证，同时保持了有竞争力的预测性能；理论预测在基准程序上得到了验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为PSC在Prime+Probe观测模型下提供了基于差分隐私的形式化安全保障，但端到端安全性（完整分支预测器在重复或自适应攻击下）仍是未来重要研究方向。

Abstract: Branch predictors improve instruction-level parallelism in modern processors and are commonly modeled using saturating counters. However, classical saturating counters are deterministic and thus vulnerable to side-channel attacks: an attacker can manipulate the counter state and infer the branch direction of a victim process. Probabilistic saturating counters (PSCs) have been proposed to mitigate this leakage by randomizing counter updates, but existing evaluations are mainly empirical. In this paper, we give a formal analysis based on differential privacy (DP): we model PSCs and the corresponding Prime+Probe attack strategies as probabilistic Moore machines, derive optimal attack strategies, and quantify the attacker's distinguishing power through DP. Our DP guarantee applies to the PSC primitive under the Prime+Probe observation model; end-to-end security for a full branch predictor under repeated or adaptive attacks is an important direction for future work. We then synthesize parameters for an enhanced PSC that satisfies a target pure DP guarantee. To evaluate utility, we derive the stationary misprediction rate and validate the theoretical predictions on benchmark programs. Compared to deterministic and existing probabilistic saturating counters, the synthesized PSCs provide formal security guarantees while preserving competitive prediction performance.

</details>


### [206] [On Understanding, Identifying, and Mitigating Vulnerabilities in Agentic Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10530)
*Md Jafrin Hossain,Mohammad Arif Hossain,Nirwan Ansari*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文对2023-2025年间智能体大语言模型（Agentic LLM）安全问题进行了系统性文献综述（PRISMA 2020指南），从743篇记录中筛选出85篇论文，发现攻击研究远超防御研究（比例3.9:1），感知层漏洞占主导（66%），而行动层漏洞仅占4.7%，并提出了四层漏洞分类框架及七个开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已从无状态对话界面演变为具备多步规划、工具调用和持久记忆的自主智能体。当这些智能体拥有真实世界权限（调用API、修改文件、查询数据库）时，推理环节被攻破可能引发未授权数据访问、不可逆状态变更或级联故障，但安全研究社区尚未跟上这一趋势，缺乏系统的领域现状量化分析。

Method: 采用PRISMA 2020系统综述方法，检索六个数据库，筛选743条记录，最终保留85篇2023-2025年关于智能体LLM安全的论文，并进行分类统计和映射分析。

Result: 攻击研究数量是防御研究的3.9倍；感知层漏洞（提示注入、越狱、对抗扰动）占66%的论文，而行动层漏洞（工具滥用、代码注入、沙箱逃逸）仅占4.7%，与真实风险不匹配；代码执行安全占3.5%，工具增强智能体占12%。论文提出四层分类法（感知、大脑、行动、交互层），映射13种漏洞类型，并识别出围绕"遏制"（containment）的七个开放问题。

Conclusion: 智能体LLM不安全性的根源在于架构耦合——弱隔离使得漏洞能够在各层之间传播，当前研究侧重点（感知层）与实际高风险区域（行动层）存在严重错配，亟需加强防御研究和跨层隔离机制设计。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have undergone a shift from stateless conversational interfaces to autonomous agents capable of multi-step planning, tool invocation, code execution, and maintaining persistent memory. When these agents operate with real-world privileges---calling APIs, modifying files, and querying databases---a compromised reasoning step can trigger unauthorized data access, irreversible state changes, or cascading failures, yet the security research community has not kept pace. To quantify the state of the field, we conducted a systematic literature review under PRISMA 2020 guidelines across six databases, screening 743 records and retaining 85 papers (2023--2025) on agentic LLM security. Attack research outpaces defense work by 3.9:1. Perception-layer vulnerabilities (prompt injection, jailbreaking, adversarial perturbations) dominate, accounting for 66\% of papers, while action-layer vulnerabilities (tool misuse, code injection, sandbox escape) appear in only 4.7\%, misaligned with real-world risk. Code execution security accounts for 3.5\%, and tool-augmented agents 12\%. We contribute a four-layer taxonomy mapping 13 vulnerability types across perception, brain, action, and interaction layers, and identify seven open problems centered on containment. Agentic LLM insecurity stems from architectural coupling, where weak isolation allows vulnerabilities to propagate across layers.

</details>


### [207] [Trigger the Straggler: Load Hijack on Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10614)
*Rui Zhang,Wenbo Jiang,Hongwei Li,Zihan Wang,Rui Zhang,Chaoshun Zuo,Jianfei Sun,Guowen Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"Load Hijack"攻击，恶意模型提供者仅修改MoE模型检查点中的路由器权重，即可将受触发流量集中调度到单个GPU上，使其成为拖后腿节点并降低整体服务性能，而普通输入的调度行为几乎不受影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家并行（EP）是大规模MoE模型服务中常用的跨GPU部署策略，路由器决策决定了token由哪些专家处理以及对应的GPU执行工作，这暴露了供应链接攻击面。本文旨在展示恶意模型提供者如何通过仅修改检查点中的路由器权重来劫持服务调度，实现触发控制的设备级性能降级攻击。

Method: Load Hijack采用三阶段优化流程：（1）设计一个目标函数，奖励触发输入集中在目标专家上；（2）解决关键冲突——直接优化该目标会导致普通输入路由也偏向相同专家；（3）通过三阶段优化，在实现强触发依赖集中性的同时保持普通输入路由接近干净参考模型的行为。

Result: 在三个MoE模型家族和四个语料库上，Load Hijack成功将92.3%到95.6%的触发token分配引导到目标专家。在实际EP服务中，触发流量使首token时间（TTFT）达到普通流量的1.43倍，吞吐量降至普通流量的0.86倍。

Conclusion: 研究证明中毒路由器可以充当触发控制的设备调度器，对实际MoE服务造成显著性能降级，这促使需要对模型检查点中的路由权重和运行时负载进行审计，以防止此类供应链攻击。

Abstract: Expert parallelism (EP) is a common strategy for serving large Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models across multiple GPUs by distributing experts among devices. Router decisions then determine both which experts process each token and which GPUs execute the resulting work. This procedure exposes a supply-chain attack surface in the serving schedule. We introduce Load Hijack, in which a malicious model provider modifies only a checkpoint's router weights, distributes the poisoned checkpoint, and retains a private trigger. When the trigger appears, the poisoned router concentrates token-to-expert assignments on experts co-located on one GPU. The resulting load makes that GPU a straggler and forces peer devices to wait, while routing on ordinary inputs remains near the clean reference. We find this conditional behavior difficult to achieve because an objective that rewards target-expert use on triggered inputs can also bias ordinary-input routing toward the same experts. To resolve this conflict, Load Hijack employs a three-stage optimization procedure that produces strong trigger-dependent concentration while keeping ordinary-input routing close to the clean reference. Across three MoE families and four corpora, Load Hijack directs 92.3% to 95.6% of triggered token assignments to the target experts. In live EP serving, triggered traffic produces 1.43x the time-to-first-token and 0.86x the throughput measured under ordinary traffic. These results show that poisoned routers can act as trigger-controlled device schedulers and motivate checkpoint audits of routing and runtime load.

</details>


### [208] [A Lightweight Fault-Detection Scheme for Barrett Modular Multiplication Using Multiple Conditional Reduction Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10736)
*Rourab Paul,Paresh Baidya,Krishnendu Guha,Amlan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于统计归约监控（SRM）的方法来保护格基后量子密码（PQC）和全同态加密（FHE）硬件加速器中的Barrett模乘（BMM）单元，以抵御侧信道攻击、硬件木马、老化等因素引发的随机和突发故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式乘法是格基PQC与FHE方案中最关键的资源、时间和能耗密集型运算。BMM作为最广泛采用的模乘方法，其硬件单元容易遭受侧信道攻击和硬件木马等恶意故障注入，以及老化等非恶意因素导致的故障，这些故障可能导致信息泄露和系统安全受损。

Method: 采用统计归约监控（SRM）方法对BMM单元进行保护，通过统计监测归约过程的正确性，实现对随机故障和突发故障的高效检测，同时保持极低的硬件开销。

Result: 所提出的SRM方法能够有效检测随机和突发故障，且硬件开销最小化，在保护BMM单元安全性的同时不影响整体性能。

Conclusion: SRM方法为BMM单元提供了一种轻量级且高效的安全防护方案，能够在最小硬件代价下保障PQC和FHE基础设施的可靠性，抵御多种内外因素导致的故障威胁。

Abstract: Polynomial multiplication is the most resource-, time-, and energy-critical operation in lattice-based Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) and Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) schemes. Lattice-based PQC schemes such as Kyber and Dilithium have already been standardized, while lattice- based FHE schemes such as BGV, BFV, and CKKS are widely recognized as leading candidate in FHE area. Barrett Modular Multiplication (BMM) for polynomial multiplication is widely adopted in PQC and FHE hardware accelerators due to its hardware friendly nature and efficient modular reduction capabilities. However, Side-Channel Attacks (SCAs) and Hardware Trojans may introduce intentional faults, while aging and various other factors can cause unintentional faults. These faults may target the BM M unit, one of the most critical components of PQC and FHE infrastructures, potentially leading to information leakage and compromising system security. In this paper, we employ a Statistical Reduction Monitoring (SRM) method to protect the BM M unit against such adversarial conditions. The proposed approach incurs minimal hardware overhead while providing efficient detection of both random and bur

</details>


### [209] [A Gateway Architecture for Enterprise MCP Authentication: Unifying Heterogeneous Auth, Identity Delegation, and the User / Non-User Persona Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10760)
*Suraj Kumar,Amy Wang,Srinivasan Manoharan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项工业界部署实践：通过构建集中式MCP网关来解决大型组织在快速采用Model Context Protocol过程中出现的认证碎片化与治理危机。该网关作为统一聚合、治理和认证层，前置所有下游MCP服务器，并在生产环境中支撑了数十个MCP服务器的运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP已成为连接LLM代理与企业工具的事实标准接口，一年内大型组织从零构建了数十个内部MCP服务器。但每个团队独立实现认证（无认证/API密钥/OAuth等），导致缺乏统一的调用者授权方式、无法追踪操作记录、也无法对离职员工进行全量账号清理，形成了治理危机。

Method: 提出集中式MCP网关架构，包含四项核心贡献：(1) 双轴认证模型（交互用户vs自动化非用户 × 凭证类型：无认证/静态动态API密钥/PKCE/客户端凭证/平台应用上下文）；(2) 网关认证层支持三种企业SSO授权和三种令牌供给模型（自带令牌、自生成令牌、基于RFC 8693令牌交换的委托OAuth）；(3) 三种端到端身份流（用户到OAuth2、非用户到服务账户、用户到服务账户）；(4) 部署演进路径从CDN/WAF/边缘防护到私有MCP隧道和企业级连接器。

Result: 该架构已在生产环境中运行，前置数十个MCP服务器，覆盖Web、桌面、自定义SDK和低代码客户端等多种终端。

Conclusion: 集中式MCP网关是解决MCP生态快速扩张中认证与治理碎片化问题的有效工业方案，为大型组织提供了可操作、可扩展的统一认证与授权架构范式。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has become the de-facto interface for connecting LLM agents to enterprise tools, and adoption has been explosive: within a year, large organizations went from zero to dozens of internally built MCP servers. That speed created a governance crisis. Each team implemented authentication independently -- some with no auth, some with API keys, some with full OAuth -- producing a fragmented landscape with no consistent way to authorize callers, track who did what, or offboard a departing employee across the fleet. This paper reports an industry deployment that resolves the crisis with a centralized MCP gateway: a single aggregation, governance, and authentication layer that fronts every downstream MCP server.
  We make four contributions grounded in production experience. First, a two-axis authentication model crossing persona (interactive user vs. automated non-user) with credential type (no-auth, static/dynamic API key, PKCE, client credentials, platform app-context). Second, a gateway authentication layer supporting three enterprise SSO grants and three token-provisioning models: Bring-Your-Own-Token, Generate-Your-Own-Token, and delegated OAuth via RFC 8693 token exchange. Third, three end-to-end identity flows -- User-to-OAuth2, Non-user-to-Service-Account, and User-to-Service-Account -- composing client, gateway, and server. Fourth, the deployment evolution from CDN/WAF/edge perimeter to private MCP tunnels and enterprise-wide connectors. The architecture is in production, fronting dozens of MCP servers across web, desktop, custom-SDK, and low-code clients.

</details>


### [210] [Strategies to Avoid Illegal Data Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11153)
*Muhammad Mubeen,Arslan Bisharat,Giri Anandhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了企业防止未经授权数据访问的三种主要方法：技术解决方案（防火墙、入侵检测系统、加密）、员工培训以及政策执行，旨在帮助企业降低数据泄露风险并维持合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着技术发展，未经授权访问数据的风险不断增加，企业需要采取主动措施保护数据安全，防止数据泄露。

Method: 本文从三个维度提出防止未经授权数据访问的方法：（1）技术解决方案——部署防火墙、入侵检测系统和加密技术；（2）员工培训——教育员工识别钓鱼邮件、可疑网站等威胁，并正确使用密码等安全措施；（3）政策执行——建立并实施数据使用准则，明确违规后果，并定期评估政策有效性。

Result: 通过结合技术解决方案、员工培训和政策执行三种手段，企业能够有效防止未经授权的数据访问，降低数据泄露风险并维持法规遵从性。

Conclusion: 防止未经授权数据访问需要多管齐下的综合策略，技术、人员与制度三者缺一不可，企业应主动采取这些措施来保障数据安全。

Abstract: For companies of all sizes, data security is a top priority. The chance of unauthorized data access increases as technology develops. To prevent unwanted access to their data, businesses must be proactive. This study examines technology solutions, personnel training, and policy enforcement as methods to prevent unauthorized data access. Data may be protected from illegal access using technological solutions like firewalls, intrusion detection systems, and encryption. Intrusion detection systems notify the administrator when suspicious behavior is found, while firewalls serve as a protective border between the internal network and the internet. Even if data is intercepted, encryption makes sure it is safe. Another effective method of avoiding unauthorized data access is employee education. Employees must be taught how to spot hazards like phishing emails and shady websites and react to them. Additionally, they should be taught the right way to utilize passwords and other security precautions. To secure data, organizations should create and implement policies. Policies should set out appropriate data and system use guidelines and provide repercussions for noncompliance. Policies should be evaluated regularly to ensure that they are current and useful. Businesses may prevent unwanted access to their data by installing technology solutions, training staff, and enforcing regulations. Organizations may reduce data breach risk and maintain regulatory compliance by taking these precautions. ...

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Closed-Loop LLM Co-Pilots for Digital Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09949)
*Serge Kernbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大型语言模型（LLM）在复杂生物系统中的应用，实现了从数据分析到自主AI引导实验的进化。该系统基于49通道植物传感器网络，通过闭环架构实现LLM对植物生理状态的评估、硬件执行器的自主控制，以及微气候优化。在三个案例研究中验证了该框架：生产中部署的智能体实现了多参数优化，最小时间模式将生产周期缩短35%，能源优化模式将能耗降低18%，并自主发现了黑暗诱导叶绿素积累的策略，实现67.9%的节能效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM从单纯的数据分析工具转变为自主实验执行者，构建AI与生物系统之间的直接闭环接口，解决数字农业中专家劳动密集和计算成本高的问题。

Method: 基于49通道植物传感器网络（多光谱、电化学、介电模态），构建LLM自主控制框架。系统处理生物物理数据，评估植物生理状态，并触发硬件执行器以优化微气候、执行表型协议或诱导受控胁迫。采用人机协作分析到自主控制的过渡架构，通过自然语言提供实时解释。

Result: 在垂直农场和单株设置中验证，生产规模的智能体完成多参数优化。相比周期性控制，最小时间模式减少35%生产周期；能源优化模式减少18%能耗，仅轻微增加培养时间；自主开发黑暗诱导叶绿素积累策略，实现67.9%的节能。

Conclusion: 该框架将LLM转变为数字农业的自主副驾驶，改善成本效益比，降低计算和专家劳动力约束，为复杂生物系统和生态系统的数据驱动探索提供了新范式。

Abstract: This study evaluates the application of Large Language Models (LLMs) in complex biological systems, evolving from data analysis to autonomous, AI-guided experimentation. The framework is driven by data from a 49-channel phytosensor network, encompassing multispectral, electrochemical, and dielectric modalities. To enhance accessibility, the system provides real-time natural-language interpretation for both specialists and non-experts. However, its core advantage lies in the transition from human-in-the-loop analysis to autonomous control. Processing biophysical data, the LLM evaluates plant physiology and triggers hardware actuators to optimize microclimates, execute phenotyping protocols, or induce controlled stress scenarios. This closed-loop architecture establishes a direct AI-biology interface, enabling data-driven exploration of complex biosystems and ecologies. The framework was validated across three case studies, based on a vertical farm and a single-plant setup and deciphered complex micro- and macro-fluctuations in plant physiology. Agents in a production-scale deployment executed multi-parameter optimization, balancing biomass accumulation, chlorophyll content, and energy consumption. The LLM processed biosensing telemetry to modulate full-spectrum, 450 nm, and 660 nm lighting at 2-hour intervals. Compared to periodic control, the system in minimal-time mode reduced the production cycle by 35%. In the energy-optimization mode, it reduced energy consumption by 18% with only a marginal increase in cultivation time, exploiting physiological inertia via light pulses. Finally, the agents autonomously developed an unforeseen strategy of dark-induced chlorophyll accumulation, resulting in a 67.9% energy saving. This framework transforms LLMs into autonomous co-pilots for digital agriculture, improving the cost-to-value ratio and lowering computational and expert-labor constraints.

</details>


### [212] [SPOTting the Future: Lookahead Explanations for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09967)
*Tamar Gozlan,Claudia V. Goldman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SPOT（采样策略观测树），一种模型无关、基于采样的深度强化学习策略解释框架，通过有限时域树结构对策略的动作偏好及其下游演化进行经验性表征，并提供渐近恢复策略最可能动作的正式保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习代理在复杂环境中表现优异，但其决策过程难以解释，现有解释方法（如单时间步特征归因）无法揭示策略的下游行为。

Method: SPOT框架在给定策略和环境模拟器访问权限的前提下，通过采样动作并递归模拟后继状态来构建可解释的有限时域树，该树经验性地表征了策略的动作偏好及其可能的下游演化。

Result: 提供了SPOT渐近恢复策略唯一最可能动作的正式保证，并刻画了其在高熵策略下的分歧行为；在SUMO-RL交通信号控制领域进行了案例研究，展示了树表示可用于检查策略偏好、比较备选未来轨迹，并揭示单时间步特征归因方法无法观察到的下游行为。

Conclusion: SPOT通过基于采样的树结构为DRL策略提供了可解释的表征，能够揭示超越单时间步归因方法的下游行为，在交通信号控制等实际领域具有可观的应用价值。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) agents achieve strong performance in complex environments, yet their decision-making processes remain difficult to interpret. We introduce SPOT (Sampling Policy Observation Tree), a novel model-agnostic, sampling-based framework for interpreting DRL policies. Given access to the policy and an environment simulator, SPOT constructs an interpretable finite-horizon tree by sampling actions and recursively simulating the resulting successor states. The tree provides an empirical representation of the policy's action preferences and their possible downstream evolution. We provide formal guarantees establishing SPOT's asymptotic recovery of the policy's unique most probable action and characterizing its disagreement behavior under high-entropy policies. We demonstrate SPOT in the SUMO-RL traffic-signal control domain. The case study illustrates how its tree-based representation can be used to inspect policy preferences, compare alternative future trajectories, and reveal downstream behaviors that are not visible through single-timestep feature-attribution methods.

</details>


### [213] [MIDAS: Mutual Information Disentanglement with Uncertainty-Aware Fusion for Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09986)
*Yuhua Wen,Yingying Zhou,Qifei Li,Yingming Gao,Zhengqi Wen,Jianhua Tao,Ya Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MIDAS框架，通过变分建模将多模态表示分解为共享和专属因子，并利用最小化/最大化互信息的minimax目标和不确定性感知融合机制，有效处理不完整多模态数据的情感分析任务，在三个数据集上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态情感分析方法假设输入模态完整，但真实场景中常出现模态缺失或不完整。已有方法依赖数据插补和启发式协调约束，无法有效提取和利用不完整多模态数据中与任务相关的信息。

Method: MIDAS框架采用变分建模策略，将每个模态表示为多元高斯潜变量，并分解为共享因子和专属因子。设计minimax目标：最小化共享与专属空间之间的互信息以实现稳定解耦，同时最大化跨模态共享空间之间的互信息以增强语义对齐。此外引入不确定性感知融合机制，利用后验方差作为可靠性指标，自适应地对潜特征加权融合。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的数据集上进行大量实验，MIDAS在各种不完整设置下相比竞争基线均取得强劲且一致的性能提升，验证了其在不完整数据场景下的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MIDAS通过互信息解耦和不确定性感知融合，有效解决了不完整多模态情感分析中的表征重构与信息利用问题，为不完整多模态场景提供了稳健且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Most existing multimodal sentiment analysis approaches assume access to complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world applications frequently encounter incomplete or corrupted modalities, posing a critical challenge. Although several methods have been proposed to tackle this issue, they mainly rely on data imputation and heuristic coordination constraints, which fail to effectively extract and leverage task-relevant information from the incomplete multimodal data. To address this challenge, we propose a unified framework termed Mutual Information Disentanglement with uncertainty-Aware fuSion (MIDAS), which effectively restructures multimodal representations under incomplete conditions. MIDAS adopts a variational modeling strategy to represent each modality with multivariate Gaussian latent variables and further decomposes them into shared and exclusive factors. To obtain reliable representations, we design a minimax objective that minimizes the mutual information between shared and exclusive spaces for stable disentanglement, while maximizing the mutual information among shared spaces across modalities to enhance semantic alignment. In addition, an uncertainty-aware fusion mechanism is introduced, where posterior variance is leveraged as a reliability indicator to adaptively weight latent features during fusion, ensuring robust integration even when modalities are incomplete. Extensive experiments on three widely used datasets show that MIDAS achieves strong and consistent performance gains over competitive baselines across a wide range of incomplete settings, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness for incomplete data scenarios.

</details>


### [214] [Towards Sustainable Artificial Intelligence: A Comprehensive Review and Comparative Analysis of Deep Learning Models' Carbon Footprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09998)
*Samar Garrab,Sarra Boughriou,Manel BenSassi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了绿色AI、绿色深度学习和降低AI模型环境影响的优化技术，比较了多种碳排放测量工具，并通过CPU实验环境对六个深度学习模型进行多标签分类任务的实证评估，发现训练阶段是碳排放的主要来源，且模型架构复杂度并不总是带来成比例的准确率提升，强调需在预测性能与环境成本之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI和深度学习模型的大规模部署，其高能耗和碳排放问题日益受到关注，亟需系统性地审视AI的环境影响并探索可持续的优化方案。

Method: 采用系统综述方法梳理绿色AI相关研究，比较多种碳测量工具；同时设计基于CPU的实证实验，对六个DL模型执行多标签分类任务，量化各模型碳排放并分析DL生命周期中各阶段对总排放的贡献。

Result: 训练阶段是DL模型碳排放的主要来源；模型架构复杂度增加并不必然带来相应的准确率提升，存在性能与环境成本之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 应在模型选择和AI系统设计中整合可持续性考量，在追求预测性能的同时重视环境影响评估。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) have become powerful tools for supporting and automating complex human tasks. Despite their benefits, growing attention has been directed toward their environmental implications, primarily due to their high energy demands and associated carbon emissions. This concern is particularly relevant in light of the increasing deployment of large-scale models, especially Deep Learning (DL) architectures, which provide advanced predictive capabilities but require substantial computational resources. This paper presents a systematic review of research on Green AI, Green DL, and optimization techniques aimed at reducing the environmental impact of AI models. In addition, we examine and compare several carbon measurement tools for estimating emissions generated by AI algorithms. To complement the review, we conducted an empirical evaluation using a CPU-based experimental setup, in which six DL models were implemented for a multi-label classification task. The objective was to quantify and compare their overall carbon emissions and to determine which stages of the DL lifecycle contribute most significantly to the total footprint. The results show that the training phase is the primary source of emissions. Moreover, the findings reveal that increased architectural complexity does not systematically translate into proportional accuracy gains, highlighting the importance of carefully balancing predictive performance and environmental cost. These results reinforce the need to integrate sustainability considerations into model selection and AI system design.

</details>


### [215] [ReCBM: Uncertainty-Gated Relational Reasoning for Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10004)
*An Sui,Yuzhu Li,Fuping Wu,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReCBM，一个不确定性门控的关系推理框架，通过在概念瓶颈层引入语义概念关系（共现、蕴含、排斥）并用不确定性引导关系细化，实现在概念不可靠（缺失或翻转）情况下的鲁棒推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概念瓶颈模型（CBM）变体虽在概念表示、不确定性估计和依赖建模上有所改进，但在概念状态不可靠（如缺失或翻转）时缺乏鲁棒推理能力，误导性语义证据会在瓶颈层传播，损害解释和下游预测。

Method: ReCBM在瓶颈层引入语义定义的概念关系（共现、蕴含、排斥），利用不确定性门控机制指导关系细化。通过建模概念间证据交换方式，并用不确定性调节每个概念在此过程中的贡献，实现鲁棒推理。

Result: 在多样化数据集上的实验表明，ReCBM在概念缺失和翻转场景下改善了概念与任务的恢复能力，支持不确定性感知的干预，并能提取紧凑的任务相关概念子集而不降低下游性能。

Conclusion: 通过不确定性门控的关系推理，ReCBM有效解决了CBM在不可靠概念状态下的鲁棒推理问题，同时保持了可解释性和下游任务性能。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) provide an interpretable framework by grounding predictions in human-understandable concepts, enabling semantic inspection and test-time intervention. Recent variants have improved CBMs through richer concept representations, uncertainty estimation, and dependency modeling. However, robust reasoning under unreliable concept states remains underexplored. Without such reasoning, misleading semantic evidence can propagate through the bottleneck, compromising both explanations and downstream predictions. To address this issue, we propose ReCBM, an uncertainty-gated relational reasoning framework for CBMs. ReCBM introduces semantically defined concept relations into the bottleneck and uses uncertainty to guide their refinement. By modeling co-occurrence, implication, and exclusion, ReCBM specifies how evidence is exchanged across concepts, while uncertainty modulates the contribution of each concept during this process. Experiments across diverse datasets showed that ReCBM improved concept and task recovery under missing and flipped concepts, supported uncertainty-aware intervention, and extracted compact task-relevant concept subsets without degrading downstream performance.

</details>


### [216] [Automating and Scaling Behavioral Scientific Research on AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10030)
*Soo Yong Lee,Jongha Lee,Jaewan Chun,Hyunjin Hwang,Fanchen Bu,Ziv Ben-Zion,Taekwan Kim,Denny Borsboom,Jaemin Yoo,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AEROBAT，这是首个能够自动化完成AI智能体行为科学研究的多智能体系统。该系统可针对任意目标行为自动执行完整的科研流程，包括生成假设、设计实验、行为评估、结果分析和撰写报告。在12个目标行为上，AEROBAT生成了79个假设，设计了1,240个受控实验并执行了23,512轮模拟，其中26个假设获得了中等到强程度的统计证据支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体在复杂环境中的广泛部署，理解其行为变得至关重要，但现有针对AI智能体的行为科学研究仍然高度依赖人工，费时费力，缺乏自动化手段。

Method: 提出AEROBAT多智能体系统，能够自动完成行为科学研究的完整流程：针对用户给定的目标行为，自动生成假设、设计并执行受控实验、进行行为评估、分析结果并撰写研究报告。

Result: 在12个目标行为上自动生成并测试了79个假设，总计设计1,240个受控实验并执行23,512轮模拟，其中26个假设获得中到强统计证据支持，包括若干新发现。

Conclusion: 自动化AI智能体行为科学研究能够有效补充和扩展人工研究的范围，为理解和评估AI智能体行为提供了可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: As AI agents are increasingly deployed in complex environments, understanding their behaviors becomes critical. Yet behavioral scientific research on AI agents remains manual and labor-intensive. We introduce AEROBAT, the first multi-agent system to automate behavioral scientific research on AI agents. Given an arbitrary target behavior by its user, AEROBAT automatically executes a full pipeline of behavioral scientific research---generating hypotheses about the behavior, designing and executing controlled experiments, making behavioral assessments, analyzing the results, and writing reports. For 12 target behaviors, we used AEROBAT to generate and test 79 hypotheses: designing 1,240 controlled experiments and executing 23,512 simulation rounds in total. Moderate-to-strong statistical evidence was found for 26 hypotheses, including some novel ones. In sum, our results demonstrate that automated behavioral scientific research on AI agents can complement and extend the reach of manual research.

</details>


### [217] [CHORUS: Complementary Experts for High-Coverage Testbench Stimulus Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10090)
*Hejia Zhang,Sheng Lu,Zhongming Yu,Chia-Tung Ho,Brucek Khailany,Jishen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CHORUS是一个面向硬件验证测试平台生成的后训练框架，通过分阶段SFT结合密集奖励RL产生行为多样的专家模型，再通过模型合并或进一步后训练整合互补优势，最终以4B参数模型在CVDP-ECov上达到88.0% Pass@1，超越DeepSeek-R1（671B）13.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在代码生成领域已有进展，可执行反馈比文本模仿提供更可靠的学习信号。硬件验证作为代码生成的重要应用，占现代芯片设计工作量的很大比例，高覆盖率的测试平台激励生成是关键任务。作者希望突破传统SFT到RL流水线的性能上限。

Method: CHORUS框架基于两个观察：(1) 分阶段SFT产生行为多样性不同的检查点，密集奖励RL将其转化为性能相当的强专家，但各有不同的任务级优势；(2) 这些互补优势可通过无训练模型合并或进一步后训练加以利用。最终将多个专家整合成一个4B参数模型。

Result: CHORUS在CVDP-ECov上实现88.0%的Pass@1，超越DeepSeek-R1（671B）13.5个百分点，同时仅使用4B参数。

Conclusion: 通过分阶段SFT产生多样化专家模型并整合其互补优势，可以在远超模型规模限制的情况下显著提升硬件验证代码生成的性能，验证了训练策略优化而非单纯增加模型参数的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have advanced code generation, where executable feedback provides a more reliable learning signal than textual imitation alone. Hardware verification is an important application of code generation and accounts for a substantial fraction of modern chip design effort, with high-coverage testbench stimulus generation as a key task. We present CHORUS, a post-training framework that pushes performance beyond what a conventional supervised fine-tuning (SFT)-to-reinforcement learning (RL) pipeline achieves. CHORUS builds on two observations. First, staged SFT produces behaviorally diverse checkpoints, and dense-reward RL turns them into strong experts with comparable aggregate performance but distinct task-level strengths. Second, these complementary strengths can be exploited through either training-free model merging or further post-training to outperform the best individual expert. By consolidating the resulting specialists into a single 4B model, CHORUS achieves 88.0% Pass@1 on CVDP-ECov, outperforming DeepSeek-R1 (671B) by 13.5 percentage points.

</details>


### [218] [MESA:Task-Adaptive Multi-Structure Evidence Selection for Long-Horizon Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10108)
*Beidi Zhao,Yaoqi Chen,Yuru Feng,Menghao Li,Qianxi Zhang,Baotong Lu,Jianan Lu,Zhirui Wang,Xinjiang Wang,Shusen Xu,Zengzhong Li,Xiaoxiao Li,Qi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MESA提出多结构证据选择框架，针对长时程agent的轨迹记忆问题，从多个互补的结构化记忆视图中动态选择并融合查询适配的子集，在AMA-Bench上超越最强基线8.5%，同时比全结构方案减少41%的证据token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程agent的轨迹中答案可能依赖于遥远的历史证据，但现有的多记忆系统要么为每个查询读取固定结构（导致上下文膨胀和噪声），要么路由到单一结构（无法组合互补证据）。实验显示最优记忆配置既非单一结构也非全量并集，而是随查询和任务变化的结构定制组合，这激发了对结构级动态选择的需求。

Method: MESA构建每个轨迹的五个互补结构视图，通过端到端的答案级反馈（弱监督）学习选择并融合查询特定的结构子集。为在弱监督下学习，采用harness优化，结合先验引导搜索和UCB引导调度来平衡探索与利用，服务于冻结的答案模型。

Result: 在AMA-Bench基准上，MESA超越最强基线8.5%，且相比全结构方案减少了41%的证据token消耗。

Conclusion: MESA验证了结构级动态选择的有效性：在长时程agent场景中，为每个查询自适应地组合多种结构记忆不仅能提升答案质量，还能显著降低证据token开销，为多记忆系统提供了更高效的设计范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents accumulate trajectories spanning hundreds of interleaved reasoning, action, and observation steps, where answering a query may depend on evidence buried far back in the history. External memory stores such trajectories as structured representations, yet each structure provides a distinct and incomplete view. Existing multi-memory systems either read a fixed set of structures for every query, inflating context and introducing noise, or route each query to a single structure, preventing the composition of complementary evidence. A controlled analysis on AMA-Bench shows that the optimal memory configuration is typically neither a single structure nor the full union, but a tailored composition of multiple structural memories that varies with query and task demands. Motivated by these findings, we formulate structure-level dynamic selection: selecting and fusing a query-adaptive subset from a library of specialized memory structures. We propose MESA (a Multi-structure Evidence Selection framework for long-horizon Agent), which builds five complementary structure views of each trajectory and learns from end-to-end answer-level feedback to select and fuse a query-specific subset for a frozen answer model. To learn under this weak supervision, MESA employs harness optimization with prior-guided search and UCB-guided scheduling to balance exploration and exploitation. On AMA-Bench, MESA outperforms the strongest baseline by 8.5% while using 41% fewer evidence tokens than the all-structure alternative.

</details>


### [219] [Generating Attacks for LLMs with GFlowNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10171)
*Berkay Ozcam,Irem Onen,Mehmet Fatih Amasyali,Emin Islam Tatli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于GFlowNets的自动化红队测试方法，利用一个大语言模型攻击另一个模型来识别漏洞，并首次引入土耳其语对抗攻击生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红队测试依赖人工测试（耗时）或预定义攻击数据集（缺乏创造力，依赖固定数据），需要一种自动化、自适应且无需人工干预的攻击生成方法。

Method: 采用GFlowNets框架，训练一个攻击者模型针对特定受害者模型进行自动化红队测试，由一个LLM生成对抗输入去攻击另一个LLM，并提供量化鲁棒性评分。

Result: 在英语环境下相比现有基准能生成更有效的对抗攻击，且作为文献中的新贡献，引入了能够生成土耳其语攻击输入的模型。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种自动化、人类独立的自适应红队测试方案，既能提升英语对抗攻击的有效性，又拓展了红队测试到土耳其语的覆盖范围。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) has facilitated their ubiquitous integration into various domains, leading to widespread adoption. However, this escalating trend has introduced significant security vulnerabilities, necessitating the identification and mitigation of flaws arising from malicious exploitation. Red teaming assessments, conducted to evaluate model robustness through diverse adversarial inputs, are essential for exposing security risks and implementing countermeasures. Currently, red teaming is performed either manually by experts or automatically using predefined attack datasets. Nevertheless, manual testing remains time-consuming, while existing automated methods suffer from limited creativity due to their inherent dependency on fixed datasets. In this study, we propose an automated, human-independent, and adaptive approach leveraging GFlowNets to identify LLM vulnerabilities by utilizing one large language model to test another. Within this framework, an attacker model is trained against a specified victim model to perform automated red teaming and provide a quantitative robustness score. This research aims to generate more effective adversarial attacks in English compared to existing benchmarks and, as a novel contribution to the literature, introduces a model capable of generating attack inputs in the Turkish language.

</details>


### [220] [The CASE Framework: A Multi-Disciplinary Control Architecture for Governing Enterprise Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10153)
*Srinivas Telukunta,Georgios Nektarios Lilis,Lucio Baron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CASE框架，将智能体AI治理划分为四个独立问题：个体智能体的控制理论、智能体集群的复杂自适应系统理论、人机协作团队的监督控制论、以及智能体舰队的工程运营管理。通过三组实证研究揭示"涌现鸿沟"——风险在涌现层显现而能力与实践严重缺失，并给出五级成熟度模型以科学化治理评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业部署自主AI智能体的速度远超治理能力，现有方法仅用单一学科（如为确定性自动化而生的DevSecOps）来应对所有尺度的自主性，导致治理框架存在根本性结构缺陷。

Method: 提出CASE四层治理框架：控制论（个体）、复杂自适应系统（集群）、监督控制论（人机团队）、工程运营（舰队）。形式化各层并推导跨层耦合条件，识别零接触部署悖论；通过三组实证研究（82%生产故障为多层轨迹、22个生态工具缺乏涌现层覆盖、35个部署均处于最低成熟度）验证框架，并构建五级非补偿性瓶颈加权成熟度模型。

Result: 三组实证研究一致表明：绝大多数生产环境中的智能体故障源于多层耦合而非单层问题，现有生态工具对Emergence层覆盖几乎为零，所有公开部署均处于最低成熟度。由此定义"Emergence Gap"——风险在涌现层实现，但能力供给与实践均严重缺失。

Conclusion: 智能体AI治理必须从单一学科范式转向四学科协同的多层治理框架。在EU AI Act第14条将有效人工监督列为法律要求的背景下，只有满足必要变异的架构才能使监督名副其实而非流于形式。

Abstract: Enterprises are deploying autonomous AI agents faster than they can govern them, and prevailing approaches stretch a single discipline, typically DevSecOps built for deterministic automation, across every scale of agency. We argue that agentic AI governance is four problems, not one, each with a mature governing science. The CASE framework assigns Control theory to the individual agent (intent as setpoint, guardrails as feedback, evaluation as observation), complex Adaptive systems theory to agent collectives (where emergence makes single-agent assurance non-compositional), Supervisory cybernetics to human-agent teams (where the Law of Requisite Variety shows unaided human oversight fails structurally), and Engineering operations to fleets (extending error budgets to decision quality so autonomy becomes a controlled variable). We formalize each layer, derive cross-layer coupling conditions, including a zero-touch deployment paradox where excellence at one-layer strains the others, and trace twenty-plus enterprise controls to their classical constructs. Three empirical studies validate the thesis: 82 percent of documented production agent failures are multi-layer trajectories; none of 22 ecosystem tools offers full Layer 2 (emergence) coverage; and all 35 scored public deployments fall in the lowest maturity band. We name this mismatch, risk realized at the emergence layer against capability barely offered and practice absent, the Emergence Gap. A five-level maturity model with a non-compensatory bottleneck-weighted index and assessment instrument operationalizes CASE as a scientific rather than process maturity model, grounded in production enterprise agentic platforms. As EU AI Act Article 14 makes effective human oversight a legal requirement, only architectures satisfying requisite variety can make oversight real rather than ceremonial.

</details>


### [221] [Logit-Boundary Geometric Belief Interfaces and Sparse Sheaf-Enclave Protocols: A Self-Contained Substrate for Secure Network Electronic Health Record (EHR) Interoperability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10300)
*Alvin Spivey,Thomas Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种电子健康记录互操作性的边界架构——几何信念接口（GBI），以logit边界组织思想为核心，通过确定性判断基板决定生成模型的提议是否可准入、需审查或隔离。在GBI BoundaryBench v0.1基准上，Qwen3-4B模型在256个任务（768次执行）中全部被确定性隔离，零覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录互操作性是边界问题：遗留系统、生成模型、术语服务、身份系统和人工审核各自暴露丰富的内部状态，但操作层面的交换需要一个狭窄的共享接口，包含类型化声明、有界不确定性、溯源以及明确的准入或弃权。现有的互操作框架缺乏对这种模型到系统边界的确定性认证机制。

Method: 提出Geometric Belief Interface（GBI）架构，核心思想是logit边界：发现模型可提出阈值前的本地分类决策分数，但由确定性判断基板决定提议是否可准入、需审查或须隔离。GBI结合有限边界语义、局部Dirichlet证据、细胞层与映射锥诊断、咨询性几何审计图，以及去中心化密码学层围协议（DCSE）草图。并构建了冻结的合成基准GBI BoundaryBench v0.1用于评估。

Result: 在GBI BoundaryBench v0.1上，Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507在256个保留任务、三种证据模式（共768次规范执行）中，全部执行完成但无一输出被基准契约接受：369次在安全解析阶段被拒绝，399次在模式验证阶段被拒绝，零覆盖率和确定性隔离。Julia附录使用标准库验证了数值证书。

Conclusion: 框架不建立临床真理、全局表示对齐或端到端安全性，而是定义模型到系统边界上的证书产生式检查。实证结果被刻意限定范围——单一4B开放权重模型在单一冻结接口下——作为关于准入边界的证据报告，而非对LLM能力或临床安全性的普遍声明。

Abstract: Electronic health-record interoperability is a boundary problem: legacy systems, generative models, terminology services, identity systems, and human reviewers may each expose rich internal states, while operational exchange requires a narrow shared interface of typed claims, bounded uncertainty, provenance, and explicit admission or abstention. This paper details a mathematical and engineering architecture for that interface. The organizing idea is the logit boundary: a discovery model may propose pre-threshold scores over a local categorical decision, but a deterministic judgment substrate decides whether the proposal is admissible, requires review, or must be quarantined before any Fast Healthcare Interoperability Resources (FHIR) transaction is constructed. The resulting Geometric Belief Interface (GBI) combines finite boundary semantics, local Dirichlet evidence, cellular-sheaf and mapping-cone diagnostics, advisory geometric audit charts, and a Decentralized Cryptographic Sheaf-Enclave (DCSE) protocol sketch for fail-closed deployment. The framework does not establish clinical truth, global representation alignment, or end-to-end safety; it defines certificate-producing checks at a model-to-system boundary. A companion frozen synthetic benchmark, GBI BoundaryBench v0.1, evaluated Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 on 256 held-out tasks across three evidence modes (768 canonical executions). All executions completed, but none produced an output accepted by the benchmark contract: 369 were rejected during safe parsing and 399 during schema validation, yielding zero coverage and deterministic quarantine. This empirical result is deliberately narrow - one 4B open-weight model under one frozen interface - and is reported as evidence about the admission boundary, not as a general claim about LLM capability or clinical safety. A Julia appendix verifies numerical certificates using standard libraries.

</details>


### [222] [GeoForge: Non-Parametric Self-Evolving Agents for Earth-Observation Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10494)
*Xin Xiao,Jiang Zhong,Junnan Zhu,Yingchao Feng,Peijin Wang,Yidan Zhang,Kaiwen Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GeoForge是一个无需训练、自进化的框架，通过将完成的地球观测工作流转化为结构化非参数执行状态，利用三种互补记忆（工作流图记忆、动作级经验、适应技能标准操作程序）来约束操作空间并检索任务条件先验，从而提升工具规划和推理准确性，在多个地理空间基准上显著改善任务精度和轨迹质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测代理需要构建科学有效的工具工作流并基于当前地理空间证据得出结论，但受限于感知语义、产品依赖、时空兼容性和参数要求。现有代理搜索空间过大，自进化系统未能将异构EO轨迹组织为跨决策级别的可复用知识。

Method: GeoForge采用训练免费的自进化框架：首先根据感知上下文约束操作空间，然后从三种互补记忆（工作流图记忆捕捉全局操作顺序、动作级经验提供局部修正、适应技能标准操作程序保留程序和数据的束）中检索任务条件先验，引导工具执行；最终通过安全门控蒸馏过程将接地轨迹转化为可复用的执行知识，形成执行-蒸馏-复用的循环，无需更新骨干LLM。

Result: 在多个地理空间基准上的实验表明，GeoForge在多类LLM骨干上一致提升任务准确率和工具使用轨迹质量，并显著减少大多数LLM的工具规划和推理错误。

Conclusion: GeoForge通过结构化的非参数执行状态组织和跨任务的知识复用，有效解决了EO代理工作流规划的挑战，且无需训练即可持续自我进化，是一种通用且有效的框架方案。

Abstract: Earth observation (EO) agents construct scientifically valid tool workflows and ground their conclusions in current geospatial evidence. This is challenging because EO workflows are constrained by sensing semantics, product dependencies, spatial and temporal compatibility, and parameter requirements. Existing agents often search a broad operation space for each query, while recent self-evolving systems do not fully organize heterogeneous EO trajectories into reusable knowledge across different decision levels. To solve this problem, we present GeoForge, a training-free, self-evolving framework that transforms completed trajectories into a structured nonparametric execution state. GeoForge constrains the operation space according to the sensing context, then retrieves a task-conditioned prior from three complementary memories. Workflow Graph Memory captures global operation order, Action-Level Experiences provide local corrections, and the Adapted Skill Standard Operating Procedure preserves procedural and data constraints. The retrieved prior guides tool execution, while current observations remain the basis of the final answer. After each task, a safety-gated distillation process converts grounded trajectories into reusable execution knowledge for future retrieval. This execution, distillation, and reuse loop improves planning without updating the backbone LLM. Experiments on multiple geospatial benchmarks demonstrate that GeoForge consistently improves both task accuracy and tool-use trajectory quality across diverse LLM backbones, while substantially reducing tool-planning and reasoning errors for most LLMs.

</details>


### [223] [SBCO: Self-Supervised, Verifier-Grounded Harness Optimization For Planning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10157)
*Vivek Kulkarni,Sudipta Paul,Aounon Kumar,Nicholas Tzou,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SBCO（Self-supervised Block Coordinate Optimizer），一种自监督的、非自引用的智能体优化方法，通过分解的验证器库和近似块坐标上升来改进智能体的输出，在不需要人类标注和固定元智能体的条件下，以4-5.5倍更少的计算预算达到或超越自修改基线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自引用自改进方法（如达尔文/赫胥黎哥德尔机器）依赖任务能力与自我修改能力的对齐，仅适用于编码任务；对于不满足对齐条件的其他任务，自适应方法需引入计算昂贵的种群搜索或自我修改搜索。因此需要一种更廉价、适用于带显式约束的规划任务的替代方案。

Method: SBCO采用封闭循环、从经验中改进的框架，但核心是自监督而非自引用。具体地，它学习一个分解的验证器库（decomposed bank of verifiers）和智能体策略（harness policy），通过近似块坐标上升（approximate block coordinate ascent）迭代优化，利用智能体自身的分级反馈改进输出，不依赖人类标签且保持元智能体固定。

Result: 在两个领域上，SBCO以4-5.5倍更少的计算预算，匹配或超过了定制的自修改基线方法的性能。

Conclusion: SBCO证明了在不牺牲性能的前提下，通过自监督的块坐标优化替代自引用机制，可以显著降低计算成本，为受约束的规划任务提供了一种更高效的自改进方法路径。

Abstract: Self-improving agents seek to reduce the human engineering effort behind AI systems by enabling them to evolve and self-improve their performance over time. Recently, methods like the Darwin Gödel Machine and the Huxley Gödel Machine have been proposed which enable open-ended, recursive self-improvement through self-reference where a coding agent edits its own code. Such self-referential self-improvement methods require that the competence required to perform the task coincides or aligns well with the competence required for self-modification which is the case for coding tasks. For domains or tasks, which do not satisfy the alignment needed, self-referential self-improvement is not available. In such cases, it is possible to adapt the above algorithms to other tasks by removing the self-referential aspect or introducing explicit self-modification of a meta-agent -- both computationally expensive, relying on population or self-modification search over many candidate agents. For planning tasks with explicit constraints, we propose a far cheaper alternative. We introduce SBCO (Self-supervised Block Coordinate Optimizer), a verifier-grounded harness optimizer in the same closed-loop, improve-from-experience family as the Gödel-machine methods, but self-supervised rather than self-referential. Given an agentic harness, SBCO learns a decomposed bank of verifiers and a harness policy via approximate block coordinate ascent, improving the agent's outputs from its own graded feedback---with a fixed meta-agent and no human labels. Across two domains SBCO matches or exceeds a customized self-modifying baseline while using 4-5.5 times less compute budget.

</details>


### [224] [MAP-Graph: Provenance-Aware Shared Memory for Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10509)
*Yiqi Wang,Zihao Yan,Jiaqi Zhang,Zhangkai Wu,Mingkai Zheng,Zequn Sun,Yanming Zhu,Taotao Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAP-Graph，一个基于溯源感知的记忆层系统，通过类型化执行图追踪代理、来源、记忆、声明和动作之间的谱系关系，将硬性授权与分级信任分离，并在动作执行前应用风险敏感门控，在受控基准测试中显著提升任务成功率与精确决策准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型代理在长工作流中利用共享记忆复用信息时，相关证据可能因权限限制而不可用，且限制会通过推导过程传播，导致摘要可能隐藏私有、被投毒、不受信任或已撤销的来源，从而引发未授权读取或不安全操作。现有方法未能清晰区分硬性授权与分级信任，也未根据动作风险调整证据要求。

Method: MAP-Graph构建一个类型化执行图，涵盖代理、来源、记忆、声明和动作五类节点。系统通过追溯祖先关系排除权限不合格的记录，基于语义相似度和乘法路径信任对合格记忆进行重排序，并在动作执行前施加风险敏感门控，同时保留受影响的谱系用于审计。

Result: 在三个领域的受控基准测试中（每个方法2,700个合成任务），MAP-Graph达到94.96%的整体任务成功率、72.70%的精确决策准确率，以及90.22%的干净环境准确率（要求正确判定Allow而非安全干预）。消融实验分离了权限过滤、路径信任和动作门控的各自贡献，额外两个骨干网络的迁移测试也保持了精确决策和访问控制优势。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，溯源不仅是事后审计的元数据，更可以作为可操作的控制信号来提升语言模型代理的安全性和决策质量，在评估场景中验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Shared memory helps language-model agents reuse information across long workflows, yet relevant evidence may not be admissible for a particular agent or action. Because restrictions propagate through derivations, summaries can conceal private, poisoned, untrusted, or revoked sources, enabling unauthorized reads or unsafe actions. Existing approaches provide semantic retrieval, scoped access, or lineage tracking, but do not clearly separate hard authorization from graded trust or adapt evidence requirements to action risk. We introduce MAP-Graph, a provenance-aware memory layer that represents agents, sources, memories, claims, and actions in a typed execution graph. It traces ancestry, excludes permission-ineligible records, reranks eligible memories by semantic similarity and multiplicative path trust, and applies a risk-sensitive gate before action execution while retaining affected lineage for audit. On a controlled benchmark of 2,700 synthetic tasks per method across three domains, MAP-Graph achieves 94.96\% overall task success, 72.70\% exact decision accuracy, and 90.22\% in the clean setting, where success requires a correct \textsc{Allow} rather than a safe intervention. Ablations isolate the roles of permission filtering, path trust, and action gating, while transfer tests with two additional backbones preserve the exact-decision and access-control advantages. These results support provenance as an operational control signal, rather than only post-hoc audit metadata, within the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [225] [TRACE: Trustworthy Retrieval-Augmented Conversational Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10176)
*Touseef Hasan,Laila Cure,Souvika Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，一种基于检索的、约束感知的对话引擎，用于公共服务聊天系统。该框架通过双数据表示模式将用户查询解析为结构和语义约束，在下游检索中使用知识图谱等表示变体，实验表明增强检索质量能显著提高用户约束满足度并减少幻觉推荐，且缩小了不同LLM之间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共服务聊天机器人需要基于公共服务目录提供推荐，同时满足用户的明确约束。然而实际中公共服务目录嘈杂且不一致，通用LLM/AI聊天机器人经常生成不可靠推荐，引用未经证实的网络来源。因此需要研究在嘈杂异构服务目录上构建的对话系统中，检索质量对约束感知推荐的影响。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，采用检索增强的约束感知架构。具体包括：1) 将用户查询解析为结构性约束和语义性约束；2) 采用双数据表示模式（含与不含知识图谱的多种变体）；3) 使用多个开源LLM和一个专有模型进行实验评估；4) 基于州级食品储藏室目录和合成查询基准进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，加强检索质量能显著提高用户约束满足度，同时减少幻觉推荐。随着检索质量提升，不同LLM之间的性能差异缩小，使结果对模型大小的敏感度降低。

Conclusion: 检索质量是构建稳健公共服务对话系统的关键因素。该发现对公共服务领域的AI系统设计具有重要指导意义，提示应优先优化检索环节而非单纯追求更大规模的模型。

Abstract: Public service chatbots are expected to deliver recommendations from an underlying public service directory, while also making sure that the recommendations respect explicit user constraints. In practice, public service directories are noisy and inconsistent, and general-purpose large language model (LLM) or AI-based chatbots frequently generate unreliable recommendations, citing unverified sources from the web. We investigate the impact of retrieval quality on constraint-aware recommendation in public service conversational systems built over noisy and heterogeneous service directories. We propose TRACE (Trustworthy Retrieval-Augmented Conversational Engine), a retrieval-based, constraint-aware framework that parses input user queries into structural and semantic constraints for downstream retrieval, with the help of a dual data representation schema. Using a curated statewide pantry directory and a synthetic query benchmark, we evaluate multiple knowledge-representation variants with and without knowledge graphs (KGs). We experiment with several open-source LLMs and a proprietary model, showing that strengthening retrieval substantially improves user constraint satisfaction while reducing hallucinated recommendations. Performance differences across LLMs narrowed in our experiments as retrieval quality improved, making results less sensitive to model size. These findings suggest that the quality of retrieval is key for robust public service conversational systems.

</details>


### [226] [Post-Hoc Sparse Coding of Latent Communication Between Vision-Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10198)
*Di Wu,Xiaohui Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究Vision Wormhole这一潜空间通信机制的冗余性，通过后验稀疏自编码器（SAE）对冻结的激活进行压缩。实验发现，使用uint16索引/float16值的稀疏负载（每token k=4个活跃系数）可将传输字节减少128倍，且七任务平均准确率几乎不下降（49.85%→49.77%）。这表明固定容量的稠密张量通信通道存在显著的可压缩性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Wormhole允许异构视觉语言模型通过通用潜空间表示进行通信，但每条消息都以固定大小的稠密张量传输，不考虑内容所需的信息密度。作者观察到部分消息仅使用一小部分表示自由度，因此假设通信通道可以被大幅压缩，并希望量化这一冗余性。

Method: 对冻结的Vision Wormhole激活进行后验SAE拟合，在九个推理基准上测量重建质量、下游效用、特征复用和token级干预效果。提出uint16索引/float16值的稀疏负载方案，每token保留k=4个活跃系数进行比较。

Result: 128倍传输字节缩减；七任务非AIME平均准确率从49.85%微降至49.77%；4096元素字典仅使用50个有效特征；任务级活跃集的平均成对Jaccard相似度为0.906。但该结果未隔离稀疏编码与传统位置选择、精度降低、低秩结构或SAE优化效果的各自贡献。

Conclusion: 实验证明Vision Wormhole的稠密传输具有强后验可压缩性，但需要匹配负载的比较来进一步区分各压缩因素的贡献，并激励设计能使负载自适应于每条消息实际信息量的通信机制。

Abstract: Latent-space communication allows heterogeneous vision-language model agents to exchange continuous representations without serializing visual and reasoning states into text. Vision Wormhole realizes this approach by translating visual features into a universal latent representation that can be consumed by another model, but every message is transported as a dense tensor of the same size regardless of its content. A fixed-capacity dense tensor therefore need not have a fixed effective information density: some messages may use only a small fraction of the available representational degrees of freedom. This observation suggests that the communication channel may be substantially compressible. We study its redundancy by fitting a post-hoc sparse autoencoder to frozen Vision Wormhole activations and measuring reconstruction, downstream utility, feature reuse, and token-level interventions across nine reasoning benchmarks. Relative to the original float32 transport, a uint16-index/float16-value sparse payload with k=4 active coefficients per token reduces the transmitted bytes by 128x. In a single-run evaluation, the seven-task non-AIME mean accuracy changes from 49.85% to 49.77%. The fitted 4096-element dictionary uses only 50 features, and task-level active sets have a mean pairwise Jaccard similarity of 0.906. These measurements establish strong post-hoc compressibility relative to the original transport, but do not yet isolate the incremental contribution of sparse coding from position selection, reduced precision, low-rank structure, or SAE optimization effects. The results motivate matched-payload comparisons and communication mechanisms whose payload adapts to the information used by each message.

</details>


### [227] [Edge Phoneme Recognition for Children's Speech through Age-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10206)
*Matthew Arboleda,Ryan Arboleda,Sophie Haak,Sam Hjelmeset,Andrew Franck,Bingrui Yang,Jose Bustamante Ortiz,Yuanrong Shen,Joel Walsh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级儿童语音音素检测方法，通过同时预测学习者的年龄和音素序列，使94M参数模型在目标数据集上超越了317M参数的WavLM Large模型，并接近90倍参数量竞赛集成模型的表现，由此开发了可在手机上运行的PhonemeTrainer应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童语音的音素检测一直面临训练数据稀缺和儿童语音特性独特等挑战，需要一种高效且在边缘设备上可行的解决方案。

Method: 训练一个轻量级模型，同时预测学习者的年龄和音素序列，利用年龄信息作为辅助任务增强音素检测能力。

Result: 94M参数模型在DrivenData目标分布上超越了317M参数的WavLM Large模型，与90倍参数量竞赛集成模型的CER差距约为0.04。基于此开发的PhonemeTrainer应用可在大多数现代手机上运行。

Conclusion: 该方法证明辅助年龄预测任务能显著提升轻量级模型的音素检测性能，为儿童语音ASR和发音辅助应用提供了端侧处理的隐私与合规优势。

Abstract: Detecting phonemes from children's speech has historically been difficult due to the scarcity of training data, and unique characteristics of children's speech. During a phoneme detection competition, we found that training a lightweight model to predict the age of the learner, as well as the phoneme sequence, enabled a 94M-parameter model to outperform WavLM Large models (317M) on the target DrivenData distribution, and fall within approximately 0.04 CER of competition ensembles with 90 times the parameters. This has enabled the creation of PhonemeTrainer, an application that can run on most modern cellular phones. This will ultimately enable better Automated Speech Recognition (ASR) and pronunciation helper apps for children's speech, with the privacy and compliance benefits that come with edge processing.

</details>


### [228] [Mitigating Bus Bunching with Reinforcement Learning Enhanced by Semantic Stop Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10207)
*Xin Dong,Vikash V. Gayah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于LLM辅助语义停靠点表征的公交滞留控制方法，利用大语言模型离线将异构停靠点信息转化为固定语义嵌入，并将其集成到深度Q学习控制器中，无需实时LLM推理。在两条真实公交线路校准的随机仿真中，该方法相比最优Daganzo基线分别将车头时距变异性、串车事件和乘客等待时间降低32.0%、69.2%和24.0%。跨线路实验表明零样本迁移泛化有限，热启动微调可加速早期学习，但冷启动训练最终性能最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的公交滞留控制器主要依赖瞬时运行变量或线路特定停靠点标识符，这些信息无法充分反映单个停靠点的功能与运行环境背景，且限制了策略在不同线路间的复用能力。

Method: 利用大语言模型离线将异构停靠点信息（物理属性、周边活动背景、历史运营特征）转换为固定语义嵌入，并将其纳入深度Q学习控制器中；由于嵌入在离线阶段预计算，控制器运行时不需实时调用LLM。仿真实验使用两条公交线路的观测数据进行校准，并进行了跨线路迁移实验验证泛化能力。

Result: 语义控制器相比最优校准的Daganzo基线分别降低车头时距变异性32.0%、串车事件69.2%和乘客等待时间24.0%。线路特定停靠点标识符无法改善仅间隔控制器的性能，而语义停靠点信息能同时改善车头时距规律性、等待时间和滞留力度。跨线路实验显示零样本迁移的即时泛化有限，热启动微调可加速早期学习，冷启动训练最终性能最佳。

Conclusion: 语义状态表征可以补充传统运行状态信息，支持基于自适应的策略复用，为提高公交滞留控制策略在不同线路间的可迁移性提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Bus bunching degrades service regularity and increases passenger waiting in high-frequency transit. Existing reinforcement-learning-based holding controllers primarily rely on instantaneous operational variables or route-specific stop identifiers, which provide limited information about the functional and operational context of individual stops and constrain policy reuse across routes. This study introduces an LLM-assisted semantic stop representation for event-driven bus holding control. An LLM is used offline to transform heterogeneous stop information, including physical attributes, surrounding activity context, and historical operational characteristics, into fixed semantic embeddings that are incorporated into a deep Q-learning controller without requiring real-time LLM inference. Experiments are conducted in stochastic simulations calibrated with observed data from two bus routes. Compared with the best calibrated Daganzo baseline, the semantic controller reduces headway variability, bunching events, and passenger waiting time by 32.0%, 69.2%, and 24.0%, respectively. A route-specific stop identifier does not improve the spacing-only controller, whereas semantic stop information improves headway regularity, waiting time, and holding effort, providing a more favorable overall trade-off across control objectives. Cross-route experiments further show that zero-shot transfer provides limited immediate generalization, while warm-start fine-tuning accelerates early-stage learning and improves transferred policies; cold-start training nevertheless achieves the best final performance. These findings suggest that semantic state representations can complement conventional operational states and support adaptation-based policy reuse across related transit routes.

</details>


### [229] [Evaluation-Conditioned Training: Teaching Models to Generalize to Stronger Oversight Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10209)
*Alec Harris,Kasey Corra,Archie Chaudhury,Yixiong Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出评估条件训练（ECT）框架，利用自然语言对每个训练样本标注反馈保真度，在部署时通过条件化高保真监控来引导模型行为，在现有算法（如SFT、PPO）基础上作为插件使用，解决奖励不规范问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的后训练方法依赖于人类标注者和自动化奖励函数来提供反馈信号，但这些反馈往往无法忠实地捕捉我们真正想要表达的意图，即反馈信号存在"保真度"缺陷。当前方法缺乏对这种不完美反馈的显式建模和处理机制。

Method: ECT框架的核心思想是：在训练阶段，利用自然语言描述每个样本对应反馈的保真度，将其作为条件信号与训练样本捆绑；在部署阶段，让LLM针对高保真监控条件进行推理，从而引出期望行为。该方法可灵活叠加在SFT和PPO等现有算法之上。
